\displaystyle|\langle\mathcal{A}\psi_{j^{\prime},k^{\prime}},\psi_{j,k}\rangle|

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\sigma,r}\|\psi_{j^{\prime},k^{\prime}}\|_{H^{r/2+\sigma}}\|\psi_{j,k}\|_{H^{r/2-\sigma}}

\displaystyle\asymp_{\sigma,r}2^{j^{\prime}(r/2+\sigma)}\cdot 2^{j(r/2-\sigma)}=2^{(j+j^{\prime})r/2-\sigma(j-j^{\prime})}.

Hence the estimate follows. The parameter
\sigma
must satisfy
0<\sigma<\min\{\gamma-r/2,\widetilde{\gamma}+r/2\}
.

(II)

Diagonal preconditioning:

The argument follows the ideas in
[
68
, Propositions 3 and 13]
, but we provide a self-contained proof for the reader’s convenience.

We first consider
\mathcal{C}_{u}
and
\widetilde{\mathbf{C}}_{u}
. Under Assumption
2.1
(ii),
\mathcal{C}_{u}
is self-adjoint and compact on
L^{2}(\mathcal{M})
; see
[
68
, Proposition 1]
. Consequently, the bi-infinite matrix
\widetilde{\mathbf{C}}_{u}
defines a symmetric and compact operator on
\ell^{2}(\mathcal{J})
. Moreover,
[
68
, Proposition 11]
,
\mathcal{C}_{u}:H^{-r_{1}}(\mathcal{M})\to H^{r_{1}}(\mathcal{M})
is continuous, and hence

\langle\mathcal{C}_{u}v,v\rangle\leq\|\mathcal{C}_{w}v\|_{H^{r_{1}}}\|v\|_{H^{-r_{1}}}\lesssim\|v\|_{H^{-r_{1}}}^{2},\quad\forall\ v\in H^{-r_{1}}.

Combined with Assumption
2.1
(iii), this yields the norm equivalence

\langle\mathcal{C}_{u}v,v\rangle\asymp\|v\|^{2}_{H^{-r_{1}}},\quad\forall\ v\in H^{-r_{1}}.

Let
v=\widetilde{\mathbf{v}}^{\top}\widetilde{\Psi}=\sum_{\lambda\in\mathcal{J}}\widetilde{v}_{\lambda}\widetilde{\psi}_{\lambda}
. Since
\widetilde{\mathbf{C}}_{u}
is the matrix representation of
\mathcal{C}_{u}
in the biorthogonal system
(\Psi,\widetilde{\Psi})
,

\langle\mathcal{C}_{u}v,v\rangle=\langle\widetilde{\mathbf{C}}_{u}\widetilde{\mathbf{v}},\widetilde{\mathbf{v}}\rangle.

By Lemma
A.1
(iv), if
-r_{1}\in(-\gamma,\widetilde{\gamma})
, then

\|v\|^{2}_{H^{-r_{1}}}\asymp\sum_{j\geq j_{0}}\sum_{k\in\nabla_{j}}2^{-2jr_{1}}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left|\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\langle v,\psi_{j,k}}}\right\rangle}}\right|^{2}=\|\mathbf{D}^{-r_{1}}\widetilde{\mathbf{v}}\|^{2}.

Therefore, for all
\widetilde{\mathbf{v}}
with
\mathbf{D}^{-r_{1}}\widetilde{\mathbf{v}}\in\ell^{2}(\mathcal{J})
,

\langle\widetilde{\mathbf{C}}_{u}\widetilde{\mathbf{v}},\widetilde{\mathbf{v}}\rangle\asymp\|\mathbf{D}^{-r_{1}}\widetilde{\mathbf{v}}\|^{2}.

This implies uniform spectral bounds for the diagonally preconditioned matrix:

c_{-}\leq\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{D}^{r_{1}}\widetilde{\mathbf{C}}_{u}\mathbf{D}^{r_{1}})\leq\sigma_{\max}(\mathbf{D}^{r_{1}}\widetilde{\mathbf{C}}_{u}\mathbf{D}^{r_{1}})\leq c_{+}.

The same argument can be applied to
\mathbf{A}
in part (i) and
\mathbf{C}_{w}
in part (iii), provided that
r/2,-r_{2}\in(-\widetilde{\gamma},\gamma)
.

∎

Appendix B
Auxiliary Materials Section

B.1
Domain Partition

Mathematically, the six disjoint regions
D_{1}
-
D_{6}
in Figure
1
are defined as:

\displaystyle D_{1}

\displaystyle:=\bigg\{(j,j^{\prime}):J\geq j>j^{\prime}\geq 0,\ j\geq\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}+t^{\prime}-\frac{r}{2}}J+\frac{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}-(t-\frac{r}{2})}{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}+t^{\prime}-\frac{r}{2}}j^{\prime}\bigg\},

\displaystyle D_{2}

\displaystyle:=\bigg\{(j,j^{\prime}):J\geq j^{\prime}>j\geq 0,\ j^{\prime}\geq\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}+t-\frac{r}{2}}J+\frac{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}-(t^{\prime}-\frac{r}{2})}{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}+t-\frac{r}{2}}j\bigg\},

\displaystyle D_{3}

\displaystyle:=\bigg\{(j,j^{\prime}):J\geq j>j^{\prime}\geq 0,\ j\leq\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}+t^{\prime}-\frac{r}{2}}J+\frac{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}-(t-\frac{r}{2})}{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}+t^{\prime}-\frac{r}{2}}j^{\prime},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\quad j\geq\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\widetilde{d}+t^{\prime}}J+\frac{\widetilde{d}+r-t}{\widetilde{d}+t^{\prime}}j^{\prime}\bigg\},

\displaystyle D_{4}

\displaystyle:=\bigg\{(j,j^{\prime}):J\geq j^{\prime}>j\geq 0,\ j^{\prime}\leq\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}+t-\frac{r}{2}}J+\frac{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}-(t^{\prime}-\frac{r}{2})}{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}+t-\frac{r}{2}}j,

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\quad j^{\prime}\geq\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\widetilde{d}+t}J+\frac{\widetilde{d}+r-t^{\prime}}{\widetilde{d}+t}j\bigg\},

\displaystyle D_{5}

\displaystyle:=\bigg\{(j,j^{\prime}):J(t+t^{\prime}-r)-jt^{\prime}-j^{\prime}t-(j+j^{\prime})\widetilde{d}<0,\ j\leq\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\widetilde{d}+t^{\prime}}J+\frac{\widetilde{d}+r-t}{\widetilde{d}+t^{\prime}}j^{\prime},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\quad j^{\prime}\leq\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\widetilde{d}+t}J+\frac{\widetilde{d}+r-t^{\prime}}{\widetilde{d}+t}j\bigg\},

\displaystyle D_{6}

\displaystyle:=\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\{(j,j^{\prime}):J(t+t^{\prime}-r)-jt^{\prime}-j^{\prime}t-(j+j^{\prime})\widetilde{d}\geq 0}}\right\}.

B.2
Support Monotonicity and Inclusion

Lemma B.1

(Support monotonicity)
.

Let
(J,t,t^{\prime})
and
(\widetilde{J},\widetilde{t},\widetilde{t}^{\prime})
be two sets of parameters, and let
\mathrm{supp}(J,t,t^{\prime})
and
\mathrm{supp}(\widetilde{J},\widetilde{t},\widetilde{t}^{\prime})
be defined as in Definition
4.2
. Assume
\widetilde{J}\geq J,\widetilde{t}\geq t,\widetilde{t}^{\prime}\geq t^{\prime}
, and
\sigma-n/2>\max\{\widetilde{t},\widetilde{t}^{\prime}\}-r/2,\ \widetilde{d}>\sigma-n/2-r/2
. Then,

\mathrm{supp}(J,t,t^{\prime})\subset\mathrm{supp}(\widetilde{J},\widetilde{t},\widetilde{t}^{\prime}).

Proof.

By definition,

\displaystyle\mathrm{supp}(J,t,t^{\prime})

\displaystyle=\bigg\{(\lambda,\lambda^{\prime})\in\Lambda_{J}\times\Lambda_{J}:\mathrm{dist}(S_{\lambda},S_{\lambda^{\prime}})\leq\tau_{jj^{\prime}},j\leq\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}+t^{\prime}-\frac{r}{2}}J+\frac{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}-(t-\frac{r}{2})}{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}+t^{\prime}-\frac{r}{2}}j^{\prime},

\displaystyle\qquad j^{\prime}\leq\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}+t-\frac{r}{2}}J+\frac{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}-(t^{\prime}-\frac{r}{2})}{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}+t-\frac{r}{2}}j\bigg\},

where

\tau_{jj^{\prime}}=a\max\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\{2^{-\min\{j,j^{\prime}\}},2^{(J(t+t^{\prime}-r)-jt^{\prime}-j^{\prime}t-(j+j^{\prime})\widetilde{d})/(2\widetilde{d}+r)}}}\right\}.

Likewise,

\displaystyle\mathrm{supp}(\widetilde{J},\widetilde{t},\widetilde{t}^{\prime})

\displaystyle:=\bigg\{(\lambda,\lambda^{\prime})\in\Lambda_{\widetilde{J}}\times\Lambda_{\widetilde{J}}:\mathrm{dist}(S_{\lambda},S_{\lambda^{\prime}})\leq\widetilde{\tau}_{jj^{\prime}},j\leq\frac{\widetilde{t}+\widetilde{t}^{\prime}-r}{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}+\widetilde{t}^{\prime}-\frac{r}{2}}\widetilde{J}+\frac{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}-(\widetilde{t}-\frac{r}{2})}{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}+\widetilde{t}^{\prime}-\frac{r}{2}}j^{\prime},

\displaystyle\qquad j^{\prime}\leq\frac{\widetilde{t}+\widetilde{t}^{\prime}-r}{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}+\widetilde{t}-\frac{r}{2}}\widetilde{J}+\frac{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}-(\widetilde{t}^{\prime}-\frac{r}{2})}{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}+\widetilde{t}-\frac{r}{2}}j\bigg\},

with

\widetilde{\tau}_{jj^{\prime}}=a\max\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\{2^{-\min\{j,j^{\prime}\}},2^{(\widetilde{J}(\widetilde{t}+\widetilde{t}^{\prime}-r)-j\widetilde{t}^{\prime}-j^{\prime}\widetilde{t}-(j+j^{\prime})\widetilde{d})/(2\widetilde{d}+r)}}}\right\}.

Fix
(\lambda,\lambda^{\prime})\in\mathrm{supp}(J,t,t^{\prime})
; we show that
(\lambda,\lambda^{\prime})\in\mathrm{supp}(\widetilde{J},\widetilde{t},\widetilde{t}^{\prime})
. Since
J\leq\widetilde{J}
, we immediately have

(\lambda,\lambda^{\prime})\in\Lambda_{J}\times\Lambda_{J}\quad\Longrightarrow\quad(\lambda,\lambda^{\prime})\in\Lambda_{\widetilde{J}}\times\Lambda_{\widetilde{J}}.

Next, note that

J(t+t^{\prime}-r)-jt^{\prime}-j^{\prime}t-(j+j^{\prime})\widetilde{d}\leq\widetilde{J}(\widetilde{t}+\widetilde{t}^{\prime}-r)-j\widetilde{t}^{\prime}-j^{\prime}\widetilde{t}-(j+j^{\prime})\widetilde{d},

and hence
\tau_{jj^{\prime}}\leq\widetilde{\tau}_{jj^{\prime}}
. Consequently,

\mathrm{dist}(S_{\lambda},S_{\lambda^{\prime}})\leq\tau_{jj^{\prime}}\quad\Longrightarrow\quad\mathrm{dist}(S_{\lambda},S_{\lambda^{\prime}})\leq\widetilde{\tau}_{jj^{\prime}}.

Moreover,

j\leq\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}+t^{\prime}-\frac{r}{2}}J+\frac{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}-(t-\frac{r}{2})}{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}+t^{\prime}-\frac{r}{2}}j^{\prime}\quad\Longrightarrow\quad j\leq\frac{\widetilde{t}+\widetilde{t}^{\prime}-r}{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}+\widetilde{t}^{\prime}-\frac{r}{2}}\widetilde{J}+\frac{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}-(\widetilde{t}-\frac{r}{2})}{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}+\widetilde{t}^{\prime}-\frac{r}{2}}j^{\prime},

which follows from

\displaystyle\bigg(\frac{\widetilde{t}+\widetilde{t}^{\prime}-r}{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}+\widetilde{t}^{\prime}-\frac{r}{2}}\widetilde{J}+\frac{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}-(\widetilde{t}-\frac{r}{2})}{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}+\widetilde{t}^{\prime}-\frac{r}{2}}j^{\prime}\bigg)-\bigg(\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}+t^{\prime}-\frac{r}{2}}J+\frac{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}-(t-\frac{r}{2})}{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}+t^{\prime}-\frac{r}{2}}j^{\prime}\bigg)

\displaystyle\geq\bigg(\frac{\widetilde{t}+\widetilde{t}^{\prime}-r}{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}+\widetilde{t}^{\prime}-\frac{r}{2}}J+\frac{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}-(\widetilde{t}-\frac{r}{2})}{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}+\widetilde{t}^{\prime}-\frac{r}{2}}j^{\prime}\bigg)-\bigg(\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}+t^{\prime}-\frac{r}{2}}J+\frac{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}-(t-\frac{r}{2})}{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}+t^{\prime}-\frac{r}{2}}j^{\prime}\bigg)

\displaystyle=\bigg(\frac{\widetilde{t}+\widetilde{t}^{\prime}-r}{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}+\widetilde{t}^{\prime}-\frac{r}{2}}-\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}+t^{\prime}-\frac{r}{2}}\bigg)(J-j^{\prime})\geq 0,

since
t\leq\widetilde{t}
,
t^{\prime}\leq\widetilde{t}^{\prime},J\leq\widetilde{J}
, and
\sigma-n/2>\max\{\widetilde{t},\widetilde{t}^{\prime}\}-r/2
. A completely analogous argument yields

j^{\prime}\leq\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}+t-\frac{r}{2}}J+\frac{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}-(t^{\prime}-\frac{r}{2})}{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}+t-\frac{r}{2}}j\quad\Longrightarrow\quad j^{\prime}\leq\frac{\widetilde{t}+\widetilde{t}^{\prime}-r}{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}+\widetilde{t}-\frac{r}{2}}\widetilde{J}+\frac{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}-(\widetilde{t}^{\prime}-\frac{r}{2})}{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}+\widetilde{t}-\frac{r}{2}}j.

Therefore,
\mathrm{supp}(J,t,t^{\prime})\subset\mathrm{supp}(\widetilde{J},\widetilde{t},\widetilde{t}^{\prime})
.
∎

Proof of Lemma
4.5
.

Let
(\lambda,\lambda^{\prime})=((j,k),(j^{\prime},k^{\prime}))
with
j>j^{\prime}
and suppose

(\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{\lambda,\lambda^{\prime}}=1
.
Then, by definition of the mask
\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})}
, the following conditions hold:

•

Distance condition:

\mathrm{dist}(S_{j,k},S_{j^{\prime},k^{\prime}})\leq\tau_{jj^{\prime}}
.

•

Scale constraint for
j^{\prime}
:

j^{\prime}\leq\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}+t-\frac{r}{2}}J+\frac{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}-(t^{\prime}-\frac{r}{2})}{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}+t-\frac{r}{2}}j
.

•

Scale constraint for
j
:

j\leq\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}+t^{\prime}-\frac{r}{2}}J+\frac{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}-(t-\frac{r}{2})}{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}+t^{\prime}-\frac{r}{2}}j^{\prime}
.

To show
(\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{\lambda^{\prime},\lambda}=1
, we must verify that

(\lambda^{\prime},\lambda)=((j^{\prime},k^{\prime}),(j,k))
also satisfies the conditions defining

\mathrm{supp}(J,t,t^{\prime})
.

•

Distance condition.

Since
\mathrm{dist}(S_{j,k},S_{j^{\prime},k^{\prime}})=\mathrm{dist}(S_{j^{\prime},k^{\prime}},S_{j,k})
, we have

\mathrm{dist}(S_{j^{\prime},k^{\prime}},S_{j,k})\leq\tau_{jj^{\prime}}\overset{(\star)}{\leq}\tau_{j^{\prime}j},

where
(\star)
follows from

\tau_{jj^{\prime}}\leq\tau_{j^{\prime}j}\quad\Longleftrightarrow\quad(-jt^{\prime}-j^{\prime}t)\leq(-j^{\prime}t^{\prime}-jt)\quad\Longleftrightarrow\quad(j^{\prime}-j)(t^{\prime}-t)\leq 0.

Since
j>j^{\prime}
and
t\leq t^{\prime}
, the last inequality holds. Thus the distance
condition for
(\lambda^{\prime},\lambda)
is satisfied.

•

Scale constraint for
j^{\prime}
.

Since
j^{\prime}<j
, it follows that

j^{\prime}<j\leq\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}+t^{\prime}-\frac{r}{2}}\,J+\frac{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}-(t-\frac{r}{2})}{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}+t^{\prime}-\frac{r}{2}}\,j,

so the required upper bound for
j^{\prime}
holds.

•

Scale constraint for
j
.

From the assumed inequality

j\leq\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}+t^{\prime}-\frac{r}{2}}\,J+\frac{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}-(t-\frac{r}{2})}{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}+t^{\prime}-\frac{r}{2}}\,j^{\prime}

and using the fact that
t\leq t^{\prime}
, we obtain

j\leq\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}+t-\frac{r}{2}}\,J+\frac{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}-(t^{\prime}-\frac{r}{2})}{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}+t-\frac{r}{2}}\,j^{\prime}.

Since all defining conditions of
\mathrm{supp}(J,t,t^{\prime})
hold for
(\lambda^{\prime},\lambda)
,
we conclude that
(\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{\lambda^{\prime},\lambda}=1
, completing the proof.
∎

Appendix C
Auxiliary Materials Section
5

C.1
Sparsity Estimates

In this subsection, we first present several basic decay estimates in Lemma
C.1
, derived from Proposition
3.2

(I)
. These bounds will be used repeatedly in the analysis of our estimator. Although similar estimates appear in various places in the literature —see, for instance,
[
51
,
45
,
68
]
— we provide here a concise and self-contained exposition for convenience. Proposition
C.2
below characterizes the sparsity pattern
\mathrm{supp}(J,t,t^{\prime})
introduced in Definition
4.2
and counts the corresponding number of nonzero entries in the compressed matrix, which plays a central role in the variance analysis. In particular, this shows that the compression achieves optimal sparsity, since
\mathrm{nnz}\big(\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})}\big)\lesssim 2^{Jn}
, which matches the intrinsic number of degrees of freedom in
V_{J}
.

Recall that, under Definition
4.2
, the thresholding parameter is given by

\tau_{jj^{\prime}}\asymp\max\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\{2^{-\min\{j,j^{\prime}\}},2^{(J(t+t^{\prime}-r)-jt^{\prime}-j^{\prime}t-(j+j^{\prime})\widetilde{d})/(2\widetilde{d}+r)}}}\right\}.

Lemma C.1

(Basic estimates)
.

(i)

For
j\geq j^{\prime}\geq j_{0}
and
\alpha>\frac{n}{n+r+2\widetilde{d}}
,

\displaystyle\sum_{k\in\nabla_{j}}|\langle\mathcal{A}\psi_{j,k},\psi_{j^{\prime},k^{\prime}}\rangle|^{\alpha}

\displaystyle\lesssim 2^{\alpha(j+j^{\prime})r/2}\cdot 2^{-(j-j^{\prime})\alpha\sigma}2^{(j-j^{\prime})n},

\displaystyle\sum_{k^{\prime}\in\nabla_{j^{\prime}}}|\langle\mathcal{A}\psi_{j,k},\psi_{j^{\prime},k^{\prime}}\rangle|^{\alpha}

\displaystyle\lesssim 2^{\alpha(j+j^{\prime})r/2}\cdot 2^{-(j-j^{\prime})\alpha\sigma}.

(ii)

For
j\geq j^{\prime}\geq j_{0}
and
\tau_{jj^{\prime}}\gtrsim 2^{-j^{\prime}}
,

\big|\{k\in\nabla_{j}:\mathrm{dist}(S_{j,k},S_{j^{\prime},k^{\prime}})\leq\tau_{jj^{\prime}}\}\big|\lesssim 2^{jn}\tau_{jj^{\prime}}^{n},

\big|\{k^{\prime}\in\nabla_{j^{\prime}}:\mathrm{dist}(S_{j,k},S_{j^{\prime},k^{\prime}})\leq\tau_{jj^{\prime}}\}\big|\lesssim 2^{j^{\prime}n}\tau_{jj^{\prime}}^{n}.

(iii)

For
j\geq j^{\prime}\geq j_{0}
,
\tau_{jj^{\prime}}\gtrsim 2^{-j^{\prime}}
and
\alpha>\frac{n}{n+r+2\widetilde{d}}
,

\sum_{k\in\nabla_{j}:\mathrm{dist}(S_{j,k},S_{j^{\prime},k^{\prime}})\geq\tau_{jj^{\prime}}}|\langle\mathcal{A}\psi_{j,k},\psi_{j^{\prime},k^{\prime}}\rangle|^{\alpha}\lesssim 2^{-\alpha(j+j^{\prime})(\widetilde{d}+n/2)}2^{jn}\tau_{jj^{\prime}}^{-\alpha(n+r+2\widetilde{d})+n},

\sum_{k^{\prime}\in\nabla_{j^{\prime}}:\mathrm{dist}(S_{j,k},S_{j^{\prime},k^{\prime}})\geq\tau_{jj^{\prime}}}|\langle\mathcal{A}\psi_{j,k},\psi_{j^{\prime},k^{\prime}}\rangle|^{\alpha}\lesssim 2^{-\alpha(j+j^{\prime})(\widetilde{d}+n/2)}2^{j^{\prime}n}\tau_{jj^{\prime}}^{-\alpha(n+r+2\widetilde{d})+n}.

Proof.

(i)

By Proposition
3.2

(I)
, for
j\geq j^{\prime}\geq j_{0}
,

|\langle\mathcal{A}\psi_{j,k},\psi_{j^{\prime},k^{\prime}}\rangle|\lesssim 2^{(j+j^{\prime})r/2}\cdot 2^{-(j-j^{\prime})\sigma}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(1+2^{j^{\prime}}\mathrm{dist}(S_{j,k},S_{j^{\prime},k^{\prime}})}}\right)^{-(n+r+2\widetilde{d})}.

where
0<\sigma<\min\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\{\gamma-r/2,\widetilde{\gamma}+r/2}}\right\}
. Therefore,

\displaystyle\sum_{k\in\nabla_{j}}|\langle\mathcal{A}\psi_{j,k},\psi_{j^{\prime},k^{\prime}}\rangle|^{\alpha}

\displaystyle\lesssim 2^{\alpha(j+j^{\prime})r/2}\cdot 2^{-(j-j^{\prime})\alpha\sigma}\sum_{k\in\nabla_{j}}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(1+2^{j^{\prime}}\mathrm{dist}(S_{j,k},S_{j^{\prime},k^{\prime}})}}\right)^{-\alpha(n+r+2\widetilde{d})}

\displaystyle=2^{\alpha(j+j^{\prime})r/2}\cdot 2^{-(j-j^{\prime})\alpha\sigma}\sum_{k\in\nabla_{j}:\mathrm{dist}(S_{j,k},S_{j^{\prime},k^{\prime}})\leq C2^{-j^{\prime}}}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(1+2^{j^{\prime}}\mathrm{dist}(S_{j,k},S_{j^{\prime},k^{\prime}})}}\right)^{-\alpha(n+r+2\widetilde{d})}

\displaystyle\quad+2^{\alpha(j+j^{\prime})r/2}\cdot 2^{-(j-j^{\prime})\alpha\sigma}\sum_{k\in\nabla_{j}:\mathrm{dist}(S_{j,k},S_{j^{\prime},k^{\prime}})\geq C2^{-j^{\prime}}}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(1+2^{j^{\prime}}\mathrm{dist}(S_{j,k},S_{j^{\prime},k^{\prime}})}}\right)^{-\alpha(n+r+2\widetilde{d})}.

Since
j\geq j^{\prime}
, there are at most
O(2^{(j-j^{\prime})n})
terms in the first sum:

\sum_{k\in\nabla_{j}:\mathrm{dist}(S_{j,k},S_{j^{\prime},k^{\prime}})\leq C2^{-j^{\prime}}}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(1+2^{j^{\prime}}\mathrm{dist}(S_{j,k},S_{j^{\prime},k^{\prime}})}}\right)^{-\alpha(n+r+2\widetilde{d})}\lesssim 2^{(j-j^{\prime})n}.

Moreover, since
\alpha(n+r+2\widetilde{d})>n
, we can estimate the second sum by an integral:

\displaystyle\sum_{k\in\nabla_{j}:\mathrm{dist}(S_{j,k},S_{j^{\prime},k^{\prime}})\geq C2^{-j^{\prime}}}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(1+2^{j^{\prime}}\mathrm{dist}(S_{j,k},S_{j^{\prime},k^{\prime}})}}\right)^{-\alpha(n+r+2\widetilde{d})}

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim 2^{-\alpha(n+r+2\widetilde{d})j^{\prime}}\cdot 2^{jn}\int_{\|x\|\geq C2^{-j^{\prime}}}\|x\|^{-\alpha(n+r+2\widetilde{d})}dx

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim 2^{-\alpha(n+r+2\widetilde{d})j^{\prime}}\cdot 2^{jn}\cdot(C2^{-j^{\prime}})^{-\alpha(n+r+2\widetilde{d})+n}

\displaystyle\qquad\asymp 2^{(j-j^{\prime})n}.

Combining the above estimates yields that

\sum_{k\in\nabla_{j}}|\langle\mathcal{A}\psi_{j,k},\psi_{j^{\prime},k^{\prime}}\rangle|^{\alpha}\lesssim 2^{\alpha(j+j^{\prime})r/2}\cdot 2^{-(j-j^{\prime})\alpha\sigma}2^{(j-j^{\prime})n}.

(ii)

By Lemma
A.1
(i), there exist fixed constants
C
and
M
such that

\mathrm{diam}(\mathrm{supp}(\psi_{j,k}))\leq C2^{-j},

and, for each
k\in\nabla_{j}
, there are at most
M
indices
k^{\prime}\in\nabla_{j}
satisfying

\mathrm{meas}(\mathrm{supp}(\psi_{j,k})\cap\mathrm{supp}(\psi_{j,k^{\prime}}))\neq 0.

Moreover, since
\tau_{jj^{\prime}}\gtrsim 2^{-\min\{j,j^{\prime}\}}
, it follows that

\big|\{k\in\nabla_{j}:\mathrm{dist}(S_{j,k},S_{j^{\prime},k^{\prime}})\leq\tau_{jj^{\prime}}\}\big|\lesssim\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\frac{\tau_{jj^{\prime}}}{2^{-j}}}}\right)^{n}=2^{jn}\tau_{jj^{\prime}}^{n},

and similarly,

\big|\{k^{\prime}\in\nabla_{j^{\prime}}:\mathrm{dist}(S_{j,k},S_{j^{\prime},k^{\prime}})\leq\tau_{jj^{\prime}}\}\big|\lesssim\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\frac{\tau_{jj^{\prime}}}{2^{-j^{\prime}}}}}\right)^{n}=2^{j^{\prime}n}\tau_{jj^{\prime}}^{n}.

(iii)

By Proposition
3.2

(I)
(i),

\displaystyle\sum_{k\in\nabla_{j}:\mathrm{dist}(S_{j,k},S_{j^{\prime},k^{\prime}})\geq\tau_{jj^{\prime}}}|\langle\mathcal{A}\psi_{j,k},\psi_{j^{\prime},k^{\prime}}\rangle|^{\alpha}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{k\in\nabla_{j}:\mathrm{dist}(S_{j,k},S_{j^{\prime},k^{\prime}})\geq\tau_{jj^{\prime}}}2^{-\alpha(j+j^{\prime})(\widetilde{d}+n/2)}\mathrm{dist}(S_{j,k},S_{j^{\prime},k^{\prime}})^{-\alpha(n+r+2\widetilde{d})}.

For
j\geq j^{\prime}\geq j_{0}
,
\tau_{jj^{\prime}}\gtrsim 2^{-j^{\prime}}=2^{-\min\{j,j^{\prime}\}}
, we can estimate the sum by an integral:

\displaystyle\lesssim 2^{-\alpha(j+j^{\prime})(\widetilde{d}+n/2)}2^{jn}\tau_{jj^{\prime}}^{-\alpha(n+r+2\widetilde{d})+n},

where we used
\alpha(n+r+2\widetilde{d})>n
in the last step. Similarly,

\sum_{k^{\prime}\in\nabla_{j^{\prime}}:\mathrm{dist}(S_{j,k},S_{j^{\prime},k^{\prime}})\geq\tau_{jj^{\prime}}}|\langle\mathcal{A}\psi_{j,k},\psi_{j^{\prime},k^{\prime}}\rangle|^{\alpha}\lesssim 2^{-\alpha(j+j^{\prime})(\widetilde{d}+n/2)}2^{j^{\prime}n}\tau_{jj^{\prime}}^{-\alpha(n+r+2\widetilde{d})+n}.

∎

Proposition C.2

(Sparsity pattern of
\mathrm{supp}(J,t,t^{\prime})
)
.

For any matrix
B
, write
\mathrm{nnz}(B):=\#\{(i,j):B_{ij}\neq 0\}
,
\mathrm{nnz}_{\mathrm{row}}(B):=\max_{i}\#\{j:B_{ij}\neq 0\}
, and
\mathrm{nnz}_{\mathrm{col}}(B):=\max_{j}\#\{i:B_{ij}\neq 0\}
. Let
\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})}\in\{0,1\}^{\Lambda_{J}\times\Lambda_{J}}
be defined as in Definition
4.2
. Then the following estimates hold.

(i)

For
(j,j^{\prime})\in D_{1}\cup D_{2}
,

\mathrm{nnz}\big((\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{j,j^{\prime}}\big)=0,\qquad\mathrm{nnz}_{\mathrm{row}}\big((\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{j,j^{\prime}}\big)=0,\qquad\mathrm{nnz}_{\mathrm{col}}\big((\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{j,j^{\prime}}\big)=0.

(ii)

For
(j,j^{\prime})\in D_{3}
,
\tau_{jj^{\prime}}\asymp 2^{-j^{\prime}}
,

\mathrm{nnz}\big((\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{j,j^{\prime}}\big)\lesssim 2^{jn},\qquad\mathrm{nnz}_{\mathrm{row}}\big((\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{j,j^{\prime}}\big)\lesssim 1,\qquad\mathrm{nnz}_{\mathrm{col}}\big((\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{j,j^{\prime}}\big)\lesssim 2^{(j-j^{\prime})n}.

(iii)

For
(j,j^{\prime})\in D_{4}
,
\tau_{jj^{\prime}}\asymp 2^{-j}
,

\mathrm{nnz}\big((\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{j,j^{\prime}}\big)\lesssim 2^{j^{\prime}n},\qquad\mathrm{nnz}_{\mathrm{row}}\big((\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{j,j^{\prime}}\big)\lesssim 2^{(j^{\prime}-j)n},\qquad\mathrm{nnz}_{\mathrm{col}}\big((\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{j,j^{\prime}}\big)\lesssim 1.

(iv)

For
(j,j^{\prime})\in D_{5}
,
\tau_{jj^{\prime}}\asymp 2^{(J(t+t^{\prime}-r)-jt^{\prime}-j^{\prime}t-(j+j^{\prime})\widetilde{d})/(2\widetilde{d}+r)}
,

\mathrm{nnz}\big((\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{j,j^{\prime}}\big)\lesssim 2^{(j+j^{\prime})n}\tau_{jj^{\prime}}^{n},\quad\mathrm{nnz}_{\mathrm{row}}\big((\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{j,j^{\prime}}\big)\lesssim 2^{j^{\prime}n}\tau_{jj^{\prime}}^{n},\quad\mathrm{nnz}_{\mathrm{col}}\big((\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{j,j^{\prime}}\big)\lesssim 2^{jn}\tau_{jj^{\prime}}^{n}.

(v)

For
(j,j^{\prime})\in D_{6}
,
(A^{\varepsilon}_{\Lambda_{J}})_{j,j^{\prime}}=(A_{\Lambda_{J}})_{j,j^{\prime}}
,

\mathrm{nnz}\big((\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{j,j^{\prime}}\big)\lesssim 2^{(j+j^{\prime})n},\qquad\mathrm{nnz}_{\mathrm{row}}\big((\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{j,j^{\prime}}\big)=2^{j^{\prime}n},\qquad\mathrm{nnz}_{\mathrm{col}}\big((\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{j,j^{\prime}}\big)\lesssim 2^{jn}.

Moreover,

\mathrm{nnz}\big(\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})}\big)\lesssim 2^{Jn},\quad\mathrm{nnz}_{\mathrm{row}}\big(\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})}\big)\lesssim 2^{Jn\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\sigma-n/2+t-r/2}},\quad\mathrm{nnz}_{\mathrm{col}}\big(\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})}\big)\lesssim 2^{Jn\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\sigma-n/2+t^{\prime}-r/2}}.

All implicit constants may depend on
n,t,t^{\prime},r,\sigma
and the wavelet system
d,\widetilde{d},\gamma,\widetilde{\gamma}
, but are independent of
j,j^{\prime},J
.

Proof.

(i) follows directly from the construction of
\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})}
in Definition
4.2
: the indicator matrix
(\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{j,j^{\prime}}=0
for
(j,j^{\prime})\in D_{1}\cup D_{2}
. For (ii)-(v), use Lemma
C.1
: for
j\geq j^{\prime}
and
\tau_{jj^{\prime}}\gtrsim 2^{-j^{\prime}}
, then

\displaystyle\big|\{k\in\nabla_{j}:\mathrm{dist}(S_{j,k},S_{j^{\prime},k^{\prime}})\leq\tau_{jj^{\prime}}\}\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim 2^{jn}\tau_{jj^{\prime}}^{n},

\displaystyle\big|\{k^{\prime}\in\nabla_{j^{\prime}}:\mathrm{dist}(S_{j,k},S_{j^{\prime},k^{\prime}})\leq\tau_{jj^{\prime}}\}\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim 2^{j^{\prime}n}\tau_{jj^{\prime}}^{n},

together with

\tau_{jj^{\prime}}\asymp\begin{cases}2^{-j^{\prime}}&\quad\text{ if }(j,j^{\prime})\in D_{3},\\
2^{-j}&\quad\text{ if }(j,j^{\prime})\in D_{4},\\
2^{(J(t+t^{\prime}-r)-jt^{\prime}-j^{\prime}t-(j+j^{\prime})\widetilde{d})/(2\widetilde{d}+r)}&\quad\text{ if }(j,j^{\prime})\in D_{5},\\
\Theta(1)&\quad\text{ if }(j,j^{\prime})\in D_{6},\\
\end{cases}

which gives the stated bounds for each region.

To prove the global bounds, we sum the blockwise estimates over
(j,j^{\prime})
in all regions. Specifically, for the
\lambda^{\prime}=(j^{\prime},k^{\prime})
-th column with
j^{\prime}\leq\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\widetilde{d}+t}J
, the number of nonzero entries is upper bounded by

\displaystyle\sum_{0\leq j\leq\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\tilde{d}+t^{\prime}}J-\frac{\tilde{d}+t}{\tilde{d}+t^{\prime}}j^{\prime}}2^{jn}+\sum_{\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\tilde{d}+t^{\prime}}J-\frac{\tilde{d}+t}{\tilde{d}+t^{\prime}}j^{\prime}\leq j\leq\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\tilde{d}+t^{\prime}}J+\frac{\tilde{d}+r-t}{\tilde{d}+t^{\prime}}j^{\prime}}2^{jn}\cdot 2^{n(J(t+t^{\prime}-r)-jt^{\prime}-j^{\prime}t-(j+j^{\prime})\widetilde{d})/(2\widetilde{d}+r)}

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\tilde{d}+t^{\prime}}J+\frac{\tilde{d}+r-t}{\tilde{d}+t^{\prime}}j^{\prime}\leq j\leq\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\sigma-n/2+t^{\prime}-r/2}J+\frac{\sigma-n/2-(t-r/2)}{\sigma-n/2+t^{\prime}-r/2}j^{\prime}}2^{(j-j^{\prime})n}

\displaystyle\lesssim 2^{n\big(\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\tilde{d}+t^{\prime}}J-\frac{\tilde{d}+t}{\tilde{d}+t^{\prime}}j^{\prime}\big)}+2^{n\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\tilde{d}+t^{\prime}}(J-j^{\prime})}+2^{n\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\sigma-n/2+t^{\prime}-r/2}(J-j^{\prime})}

\displaystyle\lesssim 2^{Jn(t+t^{\prime}-r)/(\sigma-\frac{n}{2}+t^{\prime}-\frac{r}{2})}.

For
(j^{\prime},k^{\prime})
-th column with
\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\widetilde{d}+t}J\leq j^{\prime}\leq\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}+t-\frac{r}{2}}J
, the number of nonzero entries is at most

\displaystyle\sum_{0\leq j\leq\frac{\tilde{d}+t}{\tilde{d}+r-t^{\prime}}j^{\prime}-\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\tilde{d}+r-t^{\prime}}J}1+\sum_{\frac{\tilde{d}+t}{\tilde{d}+r-t^{\prime}}j^{\prime}-\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\tilde{d}+r-t^{\prime}}J\leq j\leq\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\tilde{d}+t^{\prime}}J+\frac{\tilde{d}+r-t}{\tilde{d}+t^{\prime}}j^{\prime}}2^{jn}\cdot 2^{n(J(t+t^{\prime}-r)-jt^{\prime}-j^{\prime}t-(j+j^{\prime})\widetilde{d})/(2\widetilde{d}+r)}

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\tilde{d}+t^{\prime}}J+\frac{\tilde{d}+r-t}{\tilde{d}+t^{\prime}}j^{\prime}\leq j\leq\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\sigma-n/2+t^{\prime}-r/2}J+\frac{\sigma-n/2-(t-r/2)}{\sigma-n/2+t^{\prime}-r/2}j^{\prime}}2^{(j-j^{\prime})n}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\widetilde{d}+t}{\widetilde{d}+r-t^{\prime}}j^{\prime}-\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\widetilde{d}+r-t^{\prime}}J+2^{n\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\tilde{d}+t^{\prime}}(J-j^{\prime})}+2^{n\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\sigma-n/2+t^{\prime}-r/2}(J-j^{\prime})}

\displaystyle\lesssim 2^{Jn(t+t^{\prime}-r)/(\sigma-\frac{n}{2}+t^{\prime}-\frac{r}{2})}.

For
(j^{\prime},k^{\prime})
-th column with
\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}+t-\frac{r}{2}}J\leq j^{\prime}\leq J
, the number of nonzero entries is at most

\displaystyle\sum_{\frac{\sigma-n/2+t-r/2}{\sigma-n/2-(t^{\prime}-r/2)}j^{\prime}-\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\sigma-n/2-(t^{\prime}-r/2)}J\leq j\leq\frac{\tilde{d}+t}{\tilde{d}+r-t^{\prime}}j^{\prime}-\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\tilde{d}+r-t^{\prime}}J}1

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{\frac{\tilde{d}+t}{\tilde{d}+r-t^{\prime}}j^{\prime}-\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\tilde{d}+r-t^{\prime}}J\leq j\leq\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\tilde{d}+t^{\prime}}J+\frac{\tilde{d}+r-t}{\tilde{d}+t^{\prime}}j^{\prime}}2^{jn}\cdot 2^{n(J(t+t^{\prime}-r)-jt^{\prime}-j^{\prime}t-(j+j^{\prime})\widetilde{d})/(2\widetilde{d}+r)}

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\tilde{d}+t^{\prime}}J+\frac{\tilde{d}+r-t}{\tilde{d}+t^{\prime}}j^{\prime}\leq j\leq\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\sigma-n/2+t^{\prime}-r/2}J+\frac{\sigma-n/2-(t-r/2)}{\sigma-n/2+t^{\prime}-r/2}j^{\prime}}2^{(j-j^{\prime})n}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\widetilde{d}+t}{\widetilde{d}+r-t^{\prime}}j^{\prime}-\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\widetilde{d}+r-t^{\prime}}J-\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\frac{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}+t-\frac{r}{2}}{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}-(t^{\prime}-\frac{r}{2})}j^{\prime}-\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}-(t^{\prime}-\frac{r}{2})}J}}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+2^{n\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\tilde{d}+t^{\prime}}(J-j^{\prime})}+2^{n\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\sigma-n/2+t^{\prime}-r/2}(J-j^{\prime})}

\displaystyle\lesssim 2^{Jn(t+t^{\prime}-r)/(\sigma-\frac{n}{2}+t^{\prime}-\frac{r}{2})}.

Combining these three cases yields that

\mathrm{nnz}_{\mathrm{col}}\big(\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})}\big)\lesssim 2^{Jn(t+t^{\prime}-r)/(\sigma-\frac{n}{2}+t^{\prime}-\frac{r}{2})}.

Similarly,

\mathrm{nnz}_{\mathrm{row}}\big(\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})}\big)\lesssim 2^{Jn(t+t^{\prime}-r)/(\sigma-\frac{n}{2}+t-\frac{r}{2})}.

Finally,

\displaystyle\mathrm{nnz}\big(\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})}\big)\lesssim\sum_{j^{\prime}=0}^{J}2^{j^{\prime}n}\cdot\mathrm{nnz}((\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{\cdot,\lambda^{\prime}})

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{0\leq j^{\prime}\leq\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\tilde{d}+t}J}2^{j^{\prime}n}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(2^{n\big(\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\tilde{d}+t^{\prime}}J-\frac{\tilde{d}+t}{\tilde{d}+t^{\prime}}j^{\prime}\big)}+2^{n\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\sigma-n/2+t^{\prime}-r/2}(J-j^{\prime})}}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\tilde{d}+t}J\leq j^{\prime}\leq\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\sigma-n/2+t-r/2}J}2^{j^{\prime}n}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\frac{\widetilde{d}+t}{\widetilde{d}+r-t^{\prime}}j^{\prime}-\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\widetilde{d}+r-t^{\prime}}J+2^{n\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\sigma-n/2+t^{\prime}-r/2}(J-j^{\prime})}}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\sigma-n/2+t-r/2}J\leq j^{\prime}\leq J}2^{j^{\prime}n}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\frac{\widetilde{d}+t}{\widetilde{d}+r-t^{\prime}}j^{\prime}-\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\widetilde{d}+r-t^{\prime}}J-\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\frac{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}+t-\frac{r}{2}}{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}-(t^{\prime}-\frac{r}{2})}j^{\prime}-\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}-(t^{\prime}-\frac{r}{2})}J}}\right)}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\sigma-n/2+t-r/2}J\leq j^{\prime}\leq J}2^{j^{\prime}n}\cdot 2^{n\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\sigma-n/2+t^{\prime}-r/2}(J-j^{\prime})}

\displaystyle\lesssim 2^{Jn},

where we have used the inequality
\sum_{0\leq j^{\prime}\leq J}2^{j^{\prime}n}(J-j^{\prime})\lesssim 2^{Jn}
.
∎

C.2
Truncation

Proof of Proposition
5.9
.

We write
(\cdot)_{j,j^{\prime}}
for the
(j,j^{\prime})
wavelet block. By Lemma
C.1
(i) with
\alpha=1
, for any
j,j^{\prime}\geq j_{0}
,

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{A}_{j,j^{\prime}}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathbf{A}_{j,j^{\prime}}\|_{1}^{1/2}\;\|\mathbf{A}_{j,j^{\prime}}\|_{\infty}^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{k^{\prime}\in\nabla_{j^{\prime}}}\bigg(\sum_{k\in\nabla_{j}}|\langle\mathcal{A}\psi_{j,k},\psi_{j^{\prime},k^{\prime}}\rangle|\bigg)^{1/2}\cdot\sup_{k\in\nabla_{j}}\bigg(\sum_{k^{\prime}\in\nabla_{j^{\prime}}}|\langle\mathcal{A}\psi_{j,k},\psi_{j^{\prime},k^{\prime}}\rangle|\bigg)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(2^{(j+j^{\prime})r/2}\cdot 2^{-|j-j^{\prime}|\sigma}2^{|j-j^{\prime}|n}\cdot 2^{(j+j^{\prime})r/2}\cdot 2^{-|j-j^{\prime}|\sigma}}}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\asymp 2^{(j+j^{\prime})r/2-|j-j^{\prime}|(\sigma-n/2)}.

We now apply Lemma
C.3
to
\mathbf{D}^{-t^{\prime}}(\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{J}}^{\uparrow}-\mathbf{A})\mathbf{D}^{-t}
with the natural
(j,j^{\prime})
-block partition. Let
\mathcal{N}(\cdot)
denote the matrix of block norms. Then

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{D}^{-t^{\prime}}(\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{J}}^{\uparrow}-\mathbf{A})\mathbf{D}^{-t}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathcal{N}(\mathbf{D}^{-t^{\prime}}(\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{J}}^{\uparrow}-\mathbf{A})\mathbf{D}^{-t})\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathcal{N}(\mathbf{D}^{-t^{\prime}}(\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{J}}^{\uparrow}-\mathbf{A})\mathbf{D}^{-t})\|_{1}^{1/2}\;\|\mathcal{N}(\mathbf{D}^{-t^{\prime}}(\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{J}}^{\uparrow}-\mathbf{A})\mathbf{D}^{-t})\|_{\infty}^{1/2}.

For
j,j^{\prime}\geq j_{0}
,

\displaystyle\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\mathcal{N}(\mathbf{D}^{-t^{\prime}}(\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{J}}^{\uparrow}-\mathbf{A})\mathbf{D}^{-t})}}\right)_{j,j^{\prime}}=\|(\mathbf{D}^{-t^{\prime}}(\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{J}}^{\uparrow}-\mathbf{A})\mathbf{D}^{-t})_{j,j^{\prime}}\|=\begin{cases}0&\text{ if }j,j^{\prime}\leq J,\\
2^{-jt^{\prime}-j^{\prime}t}\|\mathbf{A}_{j,j^{\prime}}\|&\text{ otherwise}.\end{cases}

We first estimate
\|\mathcal{N}(\mathbf{D}^{-t^{\prime}}(\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{J}}^{\uparrow}-\mathbf{A})\mathbf{D}^{-t})\|_{1}
. Split into the cases
j^{\prime}\leq J
and
j^{\prime}>J
:

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{N}(\mathbf{D}^{-t^{\prime}}(\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{J}}^{\uparrow}-\mathbf{A})\mathbf{D}^{-t})\|_{1}\leq\bigg(\sup_{j^{\prime}\leq J}\sum_{j>J}2^{-jt^{\prime}-j^{\prime}t}\|\mathbf{A}_{j,j^{\prime}}\|\bigg)+\bigg(\sup_{j^{\prime}>J}\sum_{j\geq j_{0}}2^{-jt^{\prime}-j^{\prime}t}\|\mathbf{A}_{j,j^{\prime}}\|\bigg).

Case 1:
j^{\prime}\leq J
.
Using the bound for
\|\mathbf{A}_{j,j^{\prime}}\|
,

\displaystyle\sup_{j^{\prime}\leq J}\sum_{j>J}2^{-jt^{\prime}-j^{\prime}t}\|\mathbf{A}_{j,j^{\prime}}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sup_{j^{\prime}\leq J}\sum_{j>J}2^{-jt^{\prime}-j^{\prime}t}\cdot 2^{(j+j^{\prime})r/2-|j-j^{\prime}|(\sigma-n/2)}

\displaystyle=\sup_{j^{\prime}\leq J}2^{j^{\prime}(\sigma-n/2-t+r/2)}\sum_{j>J}2^{-j(\sigma-n/2+t^{\prime}-r/2)}

\displaystyle\lesssim 2^{J(\sigma-n/2-t+r/2)}\cdot 2^{-J(\sigma-n/2+t^{\prime}-r/2)}

\displaystyle=2^{-J(t+t^{\prime}-r)}.

Case 2:
j^{\prime}>J
.
Using the estimate for
\|\mathbf{A}_{j,j^{\prime}}\|
and decomposing the sum into the two regions
j_{0}\leq j\leq j^{\prime}
and
j>j^{\prime}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\sup_{j^{\prime}>J}\sum_{j\geq j_{0}}2^{-jt^{\prime}-j^{\prime}t}\|\mathbf{A}_{j,j^{\prime}}\|\lesssim\sup_{j^{\prime}>J}\sum_{j\geq j_{0}}2^{-jt^{\prime}-j^{\prime}t}\cdot 2^{(j+j^{\prime})r/2-|j-j^{\prime}|(\sigma-n/2)}

\displaystyle=\sup_{j^{\prime}>J}\bigg(\sum_{j_{0}\leq j\leq j^{\prime}}+\sum_{j>j^{\prime}}\bigg)2^{-jt^{\prime}-j^{\prime}t+(j+j^{\prime})r/2-|j-j^{\prime}|(\sigma-n/2)}

\displaystyle=\sup_{j^{\prime}>J}\bigg(\sum_{j_{0}\leq j\leq j^{\prime}}2^{j^{\prime}(-t+r/2-\sigma+n/2)}\cdot 2^{j(\sigma-n/2-(t^{\prime}-r/2))}+\sum_{j>j^{\prime}}2^{j^{\prime}(-t+r/2+\sigma-n/2)}\cdot 2^{-j(\sigma-n/2+t^{\prime}-r/2)}\bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim\sup_{j^{\prime}>J}\bigg(2^{j^{\prime}(-t+r/2-\sigma+n/2)}\cdot 2^{j^{\prime}(\sigma-n/2-(t^{\prime}-r/2))}+2^{j^{\prime}(-t+r/2+\sigma-n/2)}\cdot 2^{-j^{\prime}(\sigma-n/2+t^{\prime}-r/2)}\bigg)

\displaystyle\asymp 2^{-J(t+t^{\prime}-r)},

where we used that
\sigma-n/2>\max\{t,t^{\prime}\}-r/2\geq 0
.

Combining both cases, we conclude that

\|\mathcal{N}(\mathbf{D}^{-t^{\prime}}(\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{J}}^{\uparrow}-\mathbf{A})\mathbf{D}^{-t})\|_{1}\lesssim 2^{-J(t+t^{\prime}-r)}.

The bound for
\|\mathcal{N}(\cdot)\|_{\infty}
follows identically, yielding

\|\mathcal{N}(\mathbf{D}^{-t^{\prime}}(\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{J}}^{\uparrow}-\mathbf{A})\mathbf{D}^{-t})\|_{\infty}\lesssim 2^{-J(t+t^{\prime}-r)}.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\mathsf{Error\text{-}Truncation}

\displaystyle=\|\mathbf{D}^{-t^{\prime}}(\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{J}}^{\uparrow}-\mathbf{A})\mathbf{D}^{-t}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathcal{N}(\mathbf{D}^{-t^{\prime}}(\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{J}}^{\uparrow}-\mathbf{A})\mathbf{D}^{-t})\|_{1}^{1/2}\;\|\mathcal{N}(\mathbf{D}^{-t^{\prime}}(\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{J}}^{\uparrow}-\mathbf{A})\mathbf{D}^{-t})\|_{\infty}^{1/2}

\displaystyle\lesssim 2^{-J(t+t^{\prime}-r)}.

∎

C.3
Compression

Proof of Proposition
5.10
.

We first consider the case
t_{\ell}=t^{\prime},t_{r}=t
. Recall that

\mathsf{Error\text{-}Compression}=\|\mathbf{D}^{-t^{\prime}}_{\Lambda_{J}}(\mathbf{A}^{\varepsilon}_{\Lambda_{J}}-\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{J}})\mathbf{D}^{-t}_{\Lambda_{J}}\|.

We apply the norm-compression inequality (Lemma
C.3
) with the natural
(j,j^{\prime})
-block partition, writing
(\cdot)_{j,j^{\prime}}
for the
(j,j^{\prime})
-block:

\|\mathbf{D}^{-t^{\prime}}_{\Lambda_{J}}(\mathbf{A}^{\varepsilon}_{\Lambda_{J}}-\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{J}})\mathbf{D}^{-t}_{\Lambda_{J}}\|\leq\bigg(\sum_{j,j^{\prime}=j_{0}}^{J}2^{-2jt^{\prime}-2j^{\prime}t}\|(\mathbf{A}^{\varepsilon}_{\Lambda_{J}})_{j,j^{\prime}}-(\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{J}})_{j,j^{\prime}}\|^{2}\bigg)^{1/2}.

Next, we analyze
\|(\mathbf{A}^{\varepsilon}_{\Lambda_{J}})_{j,j^{\prime}}-(\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{J}})_{j,j^{\prime}}\|
for
j_{0}\leq j,j^{\prime}\leq J
.

Case 1.

If

j>j^{\prime}\text{ and }j\geq\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}+t^{\prime}-\frac{r}{2}}J+\frac{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}-(t-\frac{r}{2})}{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}+t^{\prime}-\frac{r}{2}}j^{\prime},

then
(\mathbf{A}^{\varepsilon}_{\Lambda_{J}})_{j,j^{\prime}}=0
, hence
\|(\mathbf{A}^{\varepsilon}_{\Lambda_{J}})_{j,j^{\prime}}-(\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{J}})_{j,j^{\prime}}\|=\|(\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{J}})_{j,j^{\prime}}\|
. Using the matrix norm inequality
\|B\|\leq\|B\|_{1}^{1/2}\|B\|_{\infty}^{1/2}
together with Lemma
C.1
(i) (with
\alpha=1
), we obtain

\displaystyle\|(\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{J}})_{j,j^{\prime}}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|(\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{J}})_{j,j^{\prime}}\|_{1}^{1/2}\cdot\|(\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{J}})_{j,j^{\prime}}\|_{\infty}^{1/2}

\displaystyle=\sup_{k^{\prime}\in\nabla_{j^{\prime}}}\bigg(\sum_{k\in\nabla_{j}}|\langle\mathcal{A}\psi_{j,k},\psi_{j^{\prime},k^{\prime}}\rangle|\bigg)^{1/2}\cdot\sup_{k\in\nabla_{j}}\bigg(\sum_{k^{\prime}\in\nabla_{j^{\prime}}}|\langle\mathcal{A}\psi_{j,k},\psi_{j^{\prime},k^{\prime}}\rangle|\bigg)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(2^{(j+j^{\prime})r/2}\cdot 2^{-(j-j^{\prime})\sigma}2^{(j-j^{\prime})n}\cdot 2^{(j+j^{\prime})r/2}\cdot 2^{-(j-j^{\prime})\sigma}}}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle=2^{(j+j^{\prime})r/2-(j-j^{\prime})(\sigma-n/2)}

\displaystyle\leq 2^{-J(t+t^{\prime}-r)+jt^{\prime}+j^{\prime}t},

where the last inequality follows from the stated slope condition:

j\geq\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}+t^{\prime}-\frac{r}{2}}J+\frac{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}-(t-\frac{r}{2})}{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}+t^{\prime}-\frac{r}{2}}j^{\prime}.

Case 2.

If

j^{\prime}>j\text{ and }j^{\prime}\geq\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}+t-\frac{r}{2}}J+\frac{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}-(t^{\prime}-\frac{r}{2})}{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}+t-\frac{r}{2}}j,

then, by the same reasoning,

\|(\mathbf{A}^{\varepsilon}_{\Lambda_{J}})_{j,j^{\prime}}-(\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{J}})_{j,j^{\prime}}\|=\|(\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{J}})_{j,j^{\prime}}\|\lesssim 2^{(j+j^{\prime})r/2-(j^{\prime}-j)(\sigma-n/2)}\lesssim 2^{-J(t+t^{\prime}-r)+jt^{\prime}+j^{\prime}t}.

Case 3.
In the remaining blocks, we set to zero all entries with
\mathrm{dist}(S_{j,k},S_{j^{\prime},k^{\prime}})>\tau_{jj^{\prime}}
. Hence

\|(\mathbf{A}^{\varepsilon}_{\Lambda_{J}})_{j,j^{\prime}}-(\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{J}})_{j,j^{\prime}}\|\leq\|(\mathbf{A}^{\varepsilon}_{\Lambda_{J}})_{j,j^{\prime}}-(\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{J}})_{j,j^{\prime}}\|_{1}^{1/2}\cdot\|(\mathbf{A}^{\varepsilon}_{\Lambda_{J}})_{j,j^{\prime}}-(\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{J}})_{j,j^{\prime}}\|_{\infty}^{1/2},

with

\displaystyle\|(\mathbf{A}^{\varepsilon}_{\Lambda_{J}})_{j,j^{\prime}}-(\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{J}})_{j,j^{\prime}}\|_{1}

\displaystyle=\sup_{k^{\prime}\in\nabla_{j^{\prime}}}\sum_{k\in\nabla_{j}:\mathrm{dist}(S_{j,k},S_{j^{\prime},k^{\prime}})\geq\tau_{jj^{\prime}}}|\langle\mathcal{A}\psi_{j,k},\psi_{j^{\prime},k^{\prime}}\rangle|

\displaystyle\lesssim 2^{-(j+j^{\prime})(\widetilde{d}+n/2)}2^{jn}\tau_{jj^{\prime}}^{-(r+2\widetilde{d})},

and

\displaystyle\|(\mathbf{A}^{\varepsilon}_{\Lambda_{J}})_{j,j^{\prime}}-(\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{J}})_{j,j^{\prime}}\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle=\sup_{k\in\nabla_{j}}\sum_{k^{\prime}\in\nabla_{j^{\prime}}:\mathrm{dist}(S_{j,k},S_{j^{\prime},k^{\prime}})\geq\tau_{jj^{\prime}}}|\langle\mathcal{A}\psi_{j,k},\psi_{j^{\prime},k^{\prime}}\rangle|

\displaystyle\lesssim 2^{-(j+j^{\prime})(\widetilde{d}+n/2)}2^{j^{\prime}n}\tau_{jj^{\prime}}^{-(r+2\widetilde{d})},

by Lemma
C.1
(iii). Therefore,

\|(\mathbf{A}^{\varepsilon}_{\Lambda_{J}})_{j,j^{\prime}}-(\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{J}})_{j,j^{\prime}}\|\lesssim 2^{-(j+j^{\prime})\widetilde{d}}\cdot\tau_{jj^{\prime}}^{-(2\widetilde{d}+r)}\lesssim 2^{-J(t+t^{\prime}-r)+jt^{\prime}+j^{\prime}t},

where the last inequality uses
\tau_{jj^{\prime}}\gtrsim 2^{(J(t+t^{\prime}-r)-jt^{\prime}-j^{\prime}t-(j+j^{\prime})\widetilde{d})/(2\widetilde{d}+r)}
.

Combining
Cases 1–3
yields,

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{D}^{-t^{\prime}}_{\Lambda_{J}}(\mathbf{A}^{\varepsilon}_{\Lambda_{J}}-\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{J}})\mathbf{D}^{-t}_{\Lambda_{J}}\|

\displaystyle\leq\bigg(\sum_{j,j^{\prime}=j_{0}}^{J}2^{-2jt^{\prime}-2j^{\prime}t}\|(\mathbf{A}^{\varepsilon}_{\Lambda_{J}})_{j,j^{\prime}}-(\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{J}})_{j,j^{\prime}}\|^{2}\bigg)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigg(\sum_{j,j^{\prime}=j_{0}}^{J}2^{-2jt^{\prime}-2j^{\prime}t}2^{-2J(t+t^{\prime}-r)+2jt^{\prime}+2j^{\prime}t}\bigg)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\lesssim J\,2^{-J(t+t^{\prime}-r)},

as claimed.

For general weights
t_{\ell}
and
t_{r}
satisfying
r/2\leq t_{\ell},t_{r}<\gamma
, we have

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{D}^{-t_{\ell}}_{\Lambda_{J}}(\mathbf{A}^{\varepsilon}_{\Lambda_{J}}-\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{J}})\mathbf{D}^{-t_{r}}_{\Lambda_{J}}\|

\displaystyle=\|\mathbf{D}^{t^{\prime}-t_{\ell}}_{\Lambda_{J}}\,\mathbf{D}^{-t^{\prime}}_{\Lambda_{J}}(\mathbf{A}^{\varepsilon}_{\Lambda_{J}}-\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{J}})\mathbf{D}^{-t}_{\Lambda_{J}}\,\mathbf{D}^{t-t_{r}}_{\Lambda_{J}}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathbf{D}^{t^{\prime}-t_{\ell}}_{\Lambda_{J}}\|\cdot\|\mathbf{D}^{-t^{\prime}}_{\Lambda_{J}}(\mathbf{A}^{\varepsilon}_{\Lambda_{J}}-\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{J}})\mathbf{D}^{-t}_{\Lambda_{J}}\|\cdot\|\mathbf{D}^{t-t_{r}}_{\Lambda_{J}}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim J\,2^{-J(t+t^{\prime}-r)}\cdot 2^{J\max\{0,t^{\prime}-t_{\ell}\}}\cdot 2^{J\max\{0,t-t_{r}\}}

\displaystyle\asymp J2^{-J\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\min\{t^{\prime},t_{\ell}\}+\min\{t,t_{r}\}-r}}\right)}.

∎

C.4
Estimation

C.4.1
Error Decomposition

Proof of Lemma
5.8
.

We recall that the estimator
\widehat{\mathbf{A}}\in\mathbb{R}^{\Lambda_{J}\times\Lambda_{J}}
in (
4.11
) is defined entrywise by

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathbf{A}}_{\lambda,\lambda^{\prime}}:=\begin{cases}(\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(1)})_{\lambda,\lambda^{\prime}}&\text{if }(\lambda,\lambda^{\prime})\in\mathrm{supp}(J,t,t^{\prime})\text{ and }j\leq j^{\prime},\\[5.69054pt]
(\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(1)})_{\lambda^{\prime},\lambda}&\text{if }(\lambda,\lambda^{\prime})\in\mathrm{supp}(J,t,t^{\prime})\text{ and }j>j^{\prime},\\[5.69054pt]
0&\text{if }(\lambda,\lambda^{\prime})\notin\mathrm{supp}(J,t,t^{\prime}).\end{cases}

Since
\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(1)}\in\mathbb{R}^{\Lambda_{\widetilde{J}}\,\times\Lambda_{J}}
is rectangular, we introduce an intermediate square matrix

\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(2)}:=(\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(1)})_{\Lambda_{J},\cdot}\in\mathbb{R}^{\Lambda_{J}\times\Lambda_{J}},

obtained by restricting
\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(1)}
to the rows indexed by
\Lambda_{J}
.

To facilitate the analysis, we decompose any matrix
\mathbf{B}\in\mathbb{R}^{\Lambda_{J}\times\Lambda_{J}}
into its
upper triangular
and
lower triangular
parts, according to the ordering of the scale indices:

\displaystyle(\mathbf{B}_{\mathrm{up}})_{\lambda,\lambda^{\prime}}:=\begin{cases}\mathbf{B}_{\lambda,\lambda^{\prime}}&j\leq j^{\prime},\\
0&j>j^{\prime},\end{cases}\qquad(\mathbf{B}_{\mathrm{low}})_{\lambda,\lambda^{\prime}}:=\begin{cases}0&j\leq j^{\prime},\\
\mathbf{B}_{\lambda,\lambda^{\prime}}&j>j^{\prime}.\end{cases}

Thus,
\mathbf{B}=\mathbf{B}_{\mathrm{up}}+\mathbf{B}_{\mathrm{low}}
. We apply this decomposition to
\widehat{\mathbf{A}},\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(2)},\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{J}},\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{J}}^{\varepsilon}
, and the mask matrix
\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})}
.

By construction in (
4.11
), the upper and lower triangular parts of
\widehat{\mathbf{A}}
satisfy

\widehat{\mathbf{A}}_{\mathrm{up}}=(\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{\mathrm{up}}\odot\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(2)},\qquad\widehat{\mathbf{A}}_{\mathrm{low}}=(\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{\mathrm{low}}\odot(\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(2)})^{\top}.

Since the compressed matrix is given by
\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{J}}^{\varepsilon}=\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})}\odot\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{J}}
, we likewise have

(\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{J}}^{\varepsilon})_{\mathrm{up}}=(\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{\mathrm{up}}\odot\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{J}},\qquad(\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{J}}^{\varepsilon})_{\mathrm{low}}=(\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{\mathrm{low}}\odot\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{J}}.

We therefore obtain

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{D}^{-t^{\prime}}_{\Lambda_{J}}(\widehat{\mathbf{A}}-\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{J}}^{\varepsilon})\mathbf{D}^{-t}_{\Lambda_{J}}\|

\displaystyle=\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|\mathbf{D}^{-t^{\prime}}_{\Lambda_{J}}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\widehat{\mathbf{A}}_{\mathrm{up}}+\widehat{\mathbf{A}}_{\mathrm{low}}-(\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{J}}^{\varepsilon})_{\mathrm{up}}-(\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{J}}^{\varepsilon})_{\mathrm{low}}}}\right)\mathbf{D}^{-t}_{\Lambda_{J}}}}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|\mathbf{D}^{-t^{\prime}}_{\Lambda_{J}}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\widehat{\mathbf{A}}_{\mathrm{up}}-(\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{J}}^{\varepsilon})_{\mathrm{up}}}}\right)\mathbf{D}^{-t}_{\Lambda_{J}}}}\right\|+\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|\mathbf{D}^{-t^{\prime}}_{\Lambda_{J}}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\widehat{\mathbf{A}}_{\mathrm{low}}-(\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{J}}^{\varepsilon})_{\mathrm{low}}}}\right)\mathbf{D}^{-t}_{\Lambda_{J}}}}\right\|

\displaystyle=\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|\mathbf{D}^{-t^{\prime}}_{\Lambda_{J}}(\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{\mathrm{up}}\odot\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(2)}-\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{J}}}}\right)\mathbf{D}^{-t}_{\Lambda_{J}}}}\right\|+\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|\mathbf{D}^{-t^{\prime}}_{\Lambda_{J}}(\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{\mathrm{low}}\odot\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left((\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(2)})^{\top}-\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{J}}}}\right)\mathbf{D}^{-t}_{\Lambda_{J}}}}\right\|

(C.1)

\displaystyle\overset{(\star)}{=}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|\mathbf{D}^{-t^{\prime}}_{\Lambda_{J}}(\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{\mathrm{up}}\odot\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(2)}-\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{J}}}}\right)\mathbf{D}^{-t}_{\Lambda_{J}}}}\right\|+\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|\mathbf{D}^{-t}_{\Lambda_{J}}((\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{\mathrm{low}})^{\top}\odot\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(2)}-\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{J}}}}\right)\mathbf{D}^{-t^{\prime}}_{\Lambda_{J}}}}\right\|,

where
(\star)
follows by transposing the second term and using
(\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{J}})^{\top}=\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{J}}
. Notice that both terms reduce to a similar structural error involving
\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(2)}-\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{J}}
.

Recall that the regression support
\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}=\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\{\lambda\in\mathcal{J}:(\lambda,\lambda^{\prime})\in\mathrm{supp}(\widetilde{J},\widetilde{t},\widetilde{t}^{\prime})}}\right\}
,
\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}^{c}=\mathcal{J}\setminus\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}
, and (
4.8
):

\displaystyle\mathbf{F}_{\cdot,\lambda^{\prime}}=\mathbf{U}\mathbf{A}_{\cdot,\lambda^{\prime}}+\mathbf{W}_{\cdot,\lambda^{\prime}}=\mathbf{U}_{\cdot,\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}\mathbf{A}_{\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}},\lambda^{\prime}}+\mathbf{U}_{\cdot,\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}^{c}}\mathbf{A}_{\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}^{c},\lambda^{\prime}}+\mathbf{W}_{\cdot,\lambda^{\prime}},

and that our preliminary estimator in
Step 1
(
4.10
) is defined columnwise by

\displaystyle(\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(1)})_{\cdot,\lambda^{\prime}}:=\begin{bmatrix}(\mathbf{U}_{\cdot,\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{\cdot,\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}})^{-1}\mathbf{U}_{\cdot,\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}^{\top}\mathbf{F}_{\cdot,\lambda^{\prime}}\\
\bm{0}\end{bmatrix}\in\mathbb{R}^{\Lambda_{\widetilde{J}}},\qquad\lambda^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{J}.

Observe that

\displaystyle(\mathbf{U}_{\cdot,\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{\cdot,\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}})^{-1}\mathbf{U}_{\cdot,\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}^{\top}\mathbf{F}_{\cdot,\lambda^{\prime}}=(\mathbf{U}_{\cdot,\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{\cdot,\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}})^{-1}\mathbf{U}_{\cdot,\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}^{\top}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\mathbf{U}_{\cdot,\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}\mathbf{A}_{\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}},\lambda^{\prime}}+\mathbf{U}_{\cdot,\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}^{c}}\mathbf{A}_{\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}^{c},\lambda^{\prime}}+\mathbf{W}_{\cdot,\lambda^{\prime}}}}\right)

\displaystyle=\mathbf{A}_{\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}},\lambda^{\prime}}+(\mathbf{U}_{\cdot,\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{\cdot,\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}})^{-1}\mathbf{U}_{\cdot,\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{\cdot,\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}^{c}}\mathbf{A}_{\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}^{c},\lambda^{\prime}}+(\mathbf{U}_{\cdot,\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{\cdot,\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}})^{-1}\mathbf{U}_{\cdot,\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{\cdot,\lambda^{\prime}}.

Hence, for each column
\lambda^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{J}
,

\displaystyle(\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(1)})_{\cdot,\lambda^{\prime}}=\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{A}_{\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}},\lambda^{\prime}}\\
\bm{0}\end{bmatrix}+\begin{bmatrix}(\mathbf{U}_{\cdot,\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{\cdot,\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}})^{-1}\mathbf{U}_{\cdot,\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{\cdot,\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}^{c}}\mathbf{A}_{\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}^{c},\lambda^{\prime}}\\
\bm{0}\end{bmatrix}+\begin{bmatrix}(\mathbf{U}_{\cdot,\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{\cdot,\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}})^{-1}\mathbf{U}_{\cdot,\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{\cdot,\lambda^{\prime}}\\
\bm{0}\end{bmatrix}.

Let
E_{\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}:\mathbb{R}^{\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}\to\mathbb{R}^{\Lambda_{\widetilde{J}}}
be the row embedding operator (pads zeros outside
\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}
). We define

\mathsf{A}:=\sum_{\lambda^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{J}}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(E_{\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}\mathbf{A}_{\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}},\lambda^{\prime}}}}\right)e_{\lambda^{\prime}}^{\top}\in\mathbb{R}^{\Lambda_{\widetilde{J}}\,\times\Lambda_{J}},

\mathsf{OVB}:=\sum_{\lambda^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{J}}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(E_{\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}(\mathbf{U}_{\cdot,\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{\cdot,\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}})^{-1}\mathbf{U}_{\cdot,\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{\cdot,\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}^{c}}\bf\mathbf{A}_{\Omega^{c}_{\lambda^{\prime}},\lambda^{\prime}}}}\right)e_{\lambda^{\prime}}^{\top}\in\mathbb{R}^{\Lambda_{\widetilde{J}}\,\times\Lambda_{J}},

and

\mathsf{Var}:=\sum_{\lambda^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{J}}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(E_{\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}(\mathbf{U}_{\cdot,\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{\cdot,\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}})^{-1}\mathbf{U}_{\cdot,\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{\cdot,\lambda^{\prime}}}}\right)e_{\lambda^{\prime}}^{\top}\in\mathbb{R}^{\Lambda_{\widetilde{J}}\,\times\Lambda_{J}},

where
e_{\lambda^{\prime}}\in\mathbb{R}^{\Lambda_{J}}
is the standard basis vector whose only nonzero entry is a
1
at coordinate
\lambda^{\prime}
. Therefore,

\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(1)}=\mathsf{A}+\mathsf{OVB}+\mathsf{Var}\in\mathbb{R}^{\Lambda_{\widetilde{J}}\,\times\Lambda_{J}}.

Taking the rows indexed by
\Lambda_{J}
, we have

\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(2)}=(\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(1)})_{\Lambda_{J},\cdot}=\mathsf{A}_{\Lambda_{J},\cdot}+\mathsf{OVB}_{\Lambda_{J},\cdot}+\mathsf{Var}_{\Lambda_{J},\cdot}\in\mathbb{R}^{\Lambda_{J}\times\Lambda_{J}}.

Because for each
\lambda^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{J}
, the support of the
\lambda^{\prime}
-th column of
M_{J,t,t^{\prime}}
is contained in
\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}
, and because
\mathsf{A}_{\cdot,\lambda^{\prime}}
coincides with
(\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{J}})_{\cdot,\lambda^{\prime}}
on
\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}
, we have

\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})}\odot(\mathsf{A}_{\Lambda_{J},\cdot}-\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{J}})=0.

Therefore,

\displaystyle(\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{\mathrm{up}}\odot\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(2)}-\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{J}}}}\right)

\displaystyle=(\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{\mathrm{up}}\odot\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\mathsf{A}_{\Lambda_{J},\cdot}+\mathsf{OVB}_{\Lambda_{J},\cdot}+\mathsf{Var}_{\Lambda_{J},\cdot}-\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{J}}}}\right)

(C.2)

\displaystyle=(\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{\mathrm{up}}\odot\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\mathsf{OVB}_{\Lambda_{J},\cdot}+\mathsf{Var}_{\Lambda_{J},\cdot}}}\right).

Moreover, by Lemma
4.5
, the support of
((\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{\mathrm{low}})^{\top}
is contained in the support of
(\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{\mathrm{up}}
. Since we already have
(\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{\mathrm{up}}\odot(\mathsf{A}_{\Lambda_{J},\cdot}-\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{J}})=0
, it follows that

((\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{\mathrm{low}})^{\top}\odot(\mathsf{A}_{\Lambda_{J},\cdot}-\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{J}})=0.

Hence,

\displaystyle((\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{\mathrm{low}})^{\top}\odot\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(2)}-\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{J}}}}\right)

\displaystyle=((\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{\mathrm{low}})^{\top}\odot\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\mathsf{A}_{\Lambda_{J},\cdot}+\mathsf{OVB}_{\Lambda_{J},\cdot}+\mathsf{Var}_{\Lambda_{J},\cdot}-\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{J}}}}\right)

(C.3)

\displaystyle=((\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{\mathrm{low}})^{\top}\odot\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\mathsf{OVB}_{\Lambda_{J},\cdot}+\mathsf{Var}_{\Lambda_{J},\cdot}}}\right).

Combining (
C.4.1
), (
C.4.1
), and (
C.4.1
), we obtain the following upper bound on the estimation error:

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{D}^{-t^{\prime}}_{\Lambda_{J}}(\widehat{\mathbf{A}}-\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{J}}^{\varepsilon})\mathbf{D}^{-t}_{\Lambda_{J}}\|

\displaystyle\leq\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|\mathbf{D}^{-t^{\prime}}_{\Lambda_{J}}(\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{\mathrm{up}}\odot\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(2)}-\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{J}}}}\right)\mathbf{D}^{-t}_{\Lambda_{J}}}}\right\|+\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|\mathbf{D}^{-t}_{\Lambda_{J}}((\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{\mathrm{low}})^{\top}\odot\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(2)}-\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{J}}}}\right)\mathbf{D}^{-t^{\prime}}_{\Lambda_{J}}}}\right\|

\displaystyle=\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|\mathbf{D}^{-t^{\prime}}_{\Lambda_{J}}(\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{\mathrm{up}}\odot\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\mathsf{OVB}_{\Lambda_{J},\cdot}+\mathsf{Var}_{\Lambda_{J},\cdot}}}\right)\mathbf{D}^{-t}_{\Lambda_{J}}}}\right\|+\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|\mathbf{D}^{-t}_{\Lambda_{J}}((\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{\mathrm{low}})^{\top}\odot\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\mathsf{OVB}_{\Lambda_{J},\cdot}+\mathsf{Var}_{\Lambda_{J},\cdot}}}\right)\mathbf{D}^{-t^{\prime}}_{\Lambda_{J}}}}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\underbrace{\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|\mathbf{D}^{-t^{\prime}}_{\Lambda_{J}}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left((\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{\mathrm{up}}\odot\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\mathsf{OVB}_{\Lambda_{J},\cdot}}}\right)}}\right)\mathbf{D}^{-t}_{\Lambda_{J}}}}\right\|+\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|\mathbf{D}^{-t}_{\Lambda_{J}}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(((\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{\mathrm{low}})^{\top}\odot\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\mathsf{OVB}_{\Lambda_{J},\cdot}}}\right)}}\right)\mathbf{D}^{-t^{\prime}}_{\Lambda_{J}}}}\right\|}_{=:\mathsf{Error\text{-}OVB}}

(C.4)

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|\mathbf{D}^{-t^{\prime}}_{\Lambda_{J}}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left((\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{\mathrm{up}}\odot\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\mathsf{Var}_{\Lambda_{J},\cdot}}}\right)}}\right)\mathbf{D}^{-t}_{\Lambda_{J}}}}\right\|+\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|\mathbf{D}^{-t}_{\Lambda_{J}}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(((\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{\mathrm{low}})^{\top}\odot\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\mathsf{Var}_{\Lambda_{J},\cdot}}}\right)}}\right)\mathbf{D}^{-t^{\prime}}_{\Lambda_{J}}}}\right\|}_{=:\mathsf{Error\text{-}Var}}.

This completes the proof.
∎

C.4.2
Omitted-Variable Bias

Proof of Proposition
5.11
.

Recall from (
5.1
) that the parameters
\widetilde{J},\widetilde{t},\widetilde{t}^{\prime}
are defined by

(C.5)

\displaystyle\widetilde{J}:=\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r+\varepsilon_{1}}{\min\{t^{\prime},r_{1}\}+t-r}J,\quad\widetilde{t}:=\max\{t,t^{\prime}\}=t^{\prime},\quad\widetilde{t}^{\prime}:=\max\{r_{1},t^{\prime}\}.

We further recall the definition of
\mathsf{Error\text{-}OVB}
from (
C.4.1
):

\displaystyle\mathsf{Error\text{-}OVB}

\displaystyle=\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|\mathbf{D}^{-t^{\prime}}_{\Lambda_{J}}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left((\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{\mathrm{up}}\odot\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\mathsf{OVB}_{\Lambda_{J},\cdot}}}\right)}}\right)\mathbf{D}^{-t}_{\Lambda_{J}}}}\right\|+\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|\mathbf{D}^{-t}_{\Lambda_{J}}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(((\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{\mathrm{low}})^{\top}\odot\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\mathsf{OVB}_{\Lambda_{J},\cdot}}}\right)}}\right)\mathbf{D}^{-t^{\prime}}_{\Lambda_{J}}}}\right\|

\displaystyle=\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|(\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{\mathrm{up}}\odot\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\mathbf{D}^{-t^{\prime}}_{\Lambda_{J}}\mathsf{OVB}_{\Lambda_{J},\cdot}\mathbf{D}^{-t}_{\Lambda_{J}}}}\right)}}\right\|+\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|((\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{\mathrm{low}})^{\top}\odot\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\mathbf{D}^{-t}_{\Lambda_{J}}\mathsf{OVB}_{\Lambda_{J},\cdot}\mathbf{D}^{-t^{\prime}}_{\Lambda_{J}}}}\right)}}\right\|,

where

\mathsf{OVB}=\sum_{\lambda^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{J}}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(E_{\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}(\mathbf{U}_{\cdot,\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{\cdot,\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}})^{-1}\mathbf{U}_{\cdot,\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{\cdot,\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}^{c}}\bf\mathbf{A}_{\Omega^{c}_{\lambda^{\prime}},\lambda^{\prime}}}}\right)e_{\lambda^{\prime}}^{\top}\in\mathbb{R}^{\Lambda_{\widetilde{J}}\,\times\Lambda_{J}}.

Define
B^{t^{\prime},t}:=\mathbf{D}_{\Lambda_{\widetilde{J}}}^{-t^{\prime}}\,\mathsf{OVB}\,\mathbf{D}_{\Lambda_{J}}^{-t}
and
B^{t,t^{\prime}}:=\mathbf{D}_{\Lambda_{\widetilde{J}}}^{-t}\,\mathsf{OVB}\,\mathbf{D}_{\Lambda_{J}}^{-t^{\prime}}
, then

(C.6)

\displaystyle\mathsf{Error\text{-}OVB}=\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|(\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{\mathrm{up}}\odot(B^{t^{\prime},t})_{\Lambda_{J},\cdot}}}\right\|+\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|((\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{\mathrm{low}})^{\top}\odot(B^{t,t^{\prime}})_{\Lambda_{J},\cdot}}}\right\|.

To simply notation, we write

\widehat{\Sigma}:=\frac{1}{N}\mathbf{U}^{\top}\mathbf{U},\quad\widehat{\Sigma}_{\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}},\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}:=\frac{1}{N}\mathbf{U}_{\cdot,\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{\cdot,\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}},\quad\widehat{\Sigma}_{\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}},\Omega^{c}_{\lambda^{\prime}}}:=\frac{1}{N}\mathbf{U}_{\cdot,\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{\cdot,\Omega^{c}_{\lambda^{\prime}}}.

We also define the corresponding population quantities
*
*

*

Here we write
\Sigma
in place of
\widetilde{\mathbf{C}}_{u}
defined in Proposition
3.2

(II)
(ii) to streamline the notation.

\Sigma:=\mathbb{E}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left[\frac{1}{N}\mathbf{U}^{\top}\mathbf{U}}}\right],\quad\Sigma_{\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}},\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}:=\mathbb{E}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left[\frac{1}{N}\mathbf{U}_{\cdot,\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{\cdot,\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}}}\right],\quad\Sigma_{\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}},\Omega^{c}_{\lambda^{\prime}}}:=\mathbb{E}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left[\frac{1}{N}\mathbf{U}_{\cdot,\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{\cdot,\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}^{c}}}}\right].

Moreover, by Proposition
3.2

(II)
(ii), let
\bar{\Sigma}=\mathbf{D}^{r_{1}}\Sigma\mathbf{D}^{r_{1}}
be the well-conditioned design covariance, which satisfies
c_{-}\leq\sigma_{\min}(\bar{\Sigma})\leq\sigma_{\max}(\bar{\Sigma})\leq c_{+}
for absolute constants
c_{-},c_{+}>0
; and its empirical version
\widehat{\bar{\Sigma}}=\mathbf{D}^{r_{1}}\widehat{\Sigma}\mathbf{D}^{r_{1}}
.

The
\lambda^{\prime}
-th column of
B^{t^{\prime},t}
can be written as

(C.7)

\displaystyle(B^{t^{\prime},t})_{\cdot,\lambda^{\prime}}=E_{\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}\mathbf{D}^{-(t^{\prime}-r_{1})}_{\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}\big(\widehat{\bar{\Sigma}}_{\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}},\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}\big)^{-1}\widehat{\bar{\Sigma}}_{\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}},\Omega^{c}_{\lambda^{\prime}}}\big(\mathbf{D}^{-r_{1}}_{\Omega^{c}_{\lambda^{\prime}}}\mathbf{A}_{\Omega^{c}_{\lambda^{\prime}},\lambda^{\prime}}\big)(\mathbf{D}_{\Lambda_{J}}^{-t})_{\lambda^{\prime},\lambda^{\prime}}\in\mathbb{R}^{\Lambda_{\widetilde{J}}},

where
(\mathbf{D}_{\Lambda_{J}}^{-t})_{\lambda^{\prime},\lambda^{\prime}}=2^{-j^{\prime}t}\in\mathbb{R}
.

Fix
\delta\in(0,1)
. By the first part of Lemma
C.5
, for every
\lambda^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{J}
, if
N\gtrsim|\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}|+\log(1/\delta)
, then with probability at least
1-\delta/2
, the matrix
\widehat{\bar{\Sigma}}_{\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}},\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}
is invertible and satisfies

(C.8)

\displaystyle\|\big(\widehat{\bar{\Sigma}}_{\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}},\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}\big)^{-1}\|=1/\sigma_{\min}(\widehat{\bar{\Sigma}}_{\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}},\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}})\lesssim 1.

We write

\widehat{\bar{\Sigma}}_{\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}},\Omega^{c}_{\lambda^{\prime}}}=\mathbf{D}^{r_{1}}_{\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}\widehat{\Sigma}_{\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}},\Omega^{c}_{\lambda^{\prime}}}\mathbf{D}^{r_{1}}_{\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}^{c}}=\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\bar{\mathbf{U}}_{i,\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}^{\top}\bar{\mathbf{U}}_{i,\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}^{c}},

where
\bar{\mathbf{U}}_{i,\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}:=\mathbf{U}_{i,\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}\mathbf{D}^{r_{1}}_{\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}},\bar{\mathbf{U}}_{i,\Omega^{c}_{\lambda^{\prime}}}:=\mathbf{U}_{i,\Omega^{c}_{\lambda^{\prime}}}\mathbf{D}^{r_{1}}_{\Omega^{c}_{\lambda^{\prime}}}
. Thus,

(C.9)

\displaystyle\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|\widehat{\bar{\Sigma}}_{\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}},\Omega^{c}_{\lambda^{\prime}}}\big(\mathbf{D}^{-r_{1}}_{\Omega^{c}_{\lambda^{\prime}}}\mathbf{A}_{\Omega^{c}_{\lambda^{\prime}},\lambda^{\prime}}\big)(\mathbf{D}_{\Lambda_{J}}^{-t^{\prime}})_{\lambda^{\prime},\lambda^{\prime}}}}\right\|=\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\bar{\mathbf{U}}_{i,\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}^{\top}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\langle\bar{\mathbf{U}}_{i,\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}^{c}},\big(\mathbf{D}^{-r_{1}}_{\Omega^{c}_{\lambda^{\prime}}}\mathbf{A}_{\Omega^{c}_{\lambda^{\prime}},\lambda^{\prime}}\big)(\mathbf{D}_{\Lambda_{J}}^{-t^{\prime}})_{\lambda^{\prime},\lambda^{\prime}}}}\right\rangle}}\right\|.

To control (
C.9
), we apply Lemma
C.4
with

X_{i}=\bar{\mathbf{U}}_{i,\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}^{\top}\in\mathbb{R}^{|\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}|},\qquad Z_{i}=\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\langle\bar{\mathbf{U}}_{i,\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}^{c}},\big(\mathbf{D}^{-r_{1}}_{\Omega^{c}_{\lambda^{\prime}}}\mathbf{A}_{\Omega^{c}_{\lambda^{\prime}},\lambda^{\prime}}\big)(\mathbf{D}_{\Lambda_{J}}^{-t^{\prime}})_{\lambda^{\prime},\lambda^{\prime}}}}\right\rangle,

and note that

\mu=\mathbb{E}[X_{i}Z_{i}]=\bar{\Sigma}_{\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}},\Omega^{c}_{\lambda^{\prime}}}\big(\mathbf{D}^{-r_{1}}_{\Omega^{c}_{\lambda^{\prime}}}\mathbf{A}_{\Omega^{c}_{\lambda^{\prime}},\lambda^{\prime}}\big)(\mathbf{D}_{\Lambda_{J}}^{-t^{\prime}})_{\lambda^{\prime},\lambda^{\prime}},

\sigma_{Z}^{2}=\mathbb{E}[Z_{i}^{2}]=\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\langle\bar{\Sigma}_{\Omega^{c}_{\lambda^{\prime}},\Omega^{c}_{\lambda^{\prime}}}\big(\mathbf{D}^{-r_{1}}_{\Omega^{c}_{\lambda^{\prime}}}\mathbf{A}_{\Omega^{c}_{\lambda^{\prime}},\lambda^{\prime}}\big)(\mathbf{D}_{\Lambda_{J}}^{-t^{\prime}})_{\lambda^{\prime},\lambda^{\prime}},\big(\mathbf{D}^{-r_{1}}_{\Omega^{c}_{\lambda^{\prime}}}\mathbf{A}_{\Omega^{c}_{\lambda^{\prime}},\lambda^{\prime}}\big)(\mathbf{D}_{\Lambda_{J}}^{-t^{\prime}})_{\lambda^{\prime},\lambda^{\prime}}}}\right\rangle,

\Sigma_{XX}=\mathbb{E}[X_{i}X_{i}^{\top}]=\bar{\Sigma}_{\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}},\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}.

It follows that, with probability at least
1-\delta/2
,

\displaystyle\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|\widehat{\bar{\Sigma}}_{\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}},\Omega^{c}_{\lambda^{\prime}}}\big(\mathbf{D}^{-r_{1}}_{\Omega^{c}_{\lambda^{\prime}}}\mathbf{A}_{\Omega^{c}_{\lambda^{\prime}},\lambda^{\prime}}\big)(\mathbf{D}_{\Lambda_{J}}^{-t^{\prime}})_{\lambda^{\prime},\lambda^{\prime}}}}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\bar{\mathbf{U}}_{i,\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\langle\bar{\mathbf{U}}_{i,\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}^{c}},\big(\mathbf{D}^{-r_{1}}_{\Omega^{c}_{\lambda^{\prime}}}\mathbf{A}_{\Omega^{c}_{\lambda^{\prime}},\lambda^{\prime}}\big)(\mathbf{D}_{\Lambda_{J}}^{-t^{\prime}})_{\lambda^{\prime},\lambda^{\prime}}}}\right\rangle}}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\big(\sigma_{Z}\|\Sigma_{XX}\|^{1/2}+\|\mu\|_{2}\big)\!\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\sqrt{\frac{|\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}|+\log(4/\delta)}{N}}+\frac{|\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}|+\log(4/\delta)}{N}}}\right)

\displaystyle\overset{(\star)}{\lesssim}\|\big(\mathbf{D}^{-r_{1}}_{\Omega^{c}_{\lambda^{\prime}}}\mathbf{A}_{\Omega^{c}_{\lambda^{\prime}},\lambda^{\prime}}\big)(\mathbf{D}_{\Lambda_{J}}^{-t^{\prime}})_{\lambda^{\prime},\lambda^{\prime}}\|_{2}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\sqrt{\frac{|\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}|+\log(4/\delta)}{N}}+\frac{|\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}|+\log(4/\delta)}{N}}}\right)

(C.10)

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\big(\mathbf{D}^{-r_{1}}_{\Omega^{c}_{\lambda^{\prime}}}\mathbf{A}_{\Omega^{c}_{\lambda^{\prime}},\lambda^{\prime}}\big)(\mathbf{D}_{\Lambda_{J}}^{-t^{\prime}})_{\lambda^{\prime},\lambda^{\prime}}\|_{2},

provided that
N\gtrsim|\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}|+\log(1/\delta)
. The inequality
(\star)
follows from the bounds

\displaystyle\sigma_{Z}=(\mathbb{E}[Z_{i}^{2}])^{1/2}

\displaystyle=\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\langle\bar{\Sigma}_{\Omega^{c}_{\lambda^{\prime}},\Omega^{c}_{\lambda^{\prime}}}\big(\mathbf{D}^{-r_{1}}_{\Omega^{c}_{\lambda^{\prime}}}\mathbf{A}_{\Omega^{c}_{\lambda^{\prime}},\lambda^{\prime}}\big)(\mathbf{D}_{\Lambda_{J}}^{-t^{\prime}})_{\lambda^{\prime},\lambda^{\prime}},\big(\mathbf{D}^{-r_{1}}_{\Omega^{c}_{\lambda^{\prime}}}\mathbf{A}_{\Omega^{c}_{\lambda^{\prime}},\lambda^{\prime}}\big)(\mathbf{D}_{\Lambda_{J}}^{-t^{\prime}})_{\lambda^{\prime},\lambda^{\prime}}}}\right\rangle^{1/2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\big(\mathbf{D}^{-r_{1}}_{\Omega^{c}_{\lambda^{\prime}}}\mathbf{A}_{\Omega^{c}_{\lambda^{\prime}},\lambda^{\prime}}\big)(\mathbf{D}_{\Lambda_{J}}^{-t^{\prime}})_{\lambda^{\prime},\lambda^{\prime}}\|_{2},\qquad\text{since }\|\bar{\Sigma}_{\Omega^{c}_{\lambda^{\prime}},\Omega^{c}_{\lambda^{\prime}}}\|\lesssim 1,

and

\|\mu\|_{2}=\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|\bar{\Sigma}_{\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}},\Omega^{c}_{\lambda^{\prime}}}\big(\mathbf{D}^{-r_{1}}_{\Omega^{c}_{\lambda^{\prime}}}\mathbf{A}_{\Omega^{c}_{\lambda^{\prime}},\lambda^{\prime}}\big)(\mathbf{D}_{\Lambda_{J}}^{-t^{\prime}})_{\lambda^{\prime},\lambda^{\prime}}}}\right\|_{2}\lesssim\|\big(\mathbf{D}^{-r_{1}}_{\Omega^{c}_{\lambda^{\prime}}}\mathbf{A}_{\Omega^{c}_{\lambda^{\prime}},\lambda^{\prime}}\big)(\mathbf{D}_{\Lambda_{J}}^{-t^{\prime}})_{\lambda^{\prime},\lambda^{\prime}}\|_{2},\quad\text{since }\|\bar{\Sigma}_{\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}},\Omega^{c}_{\lambda^{\prime}}}\|\lesssim 1.

Therefore, combining (
C.7
), (
C.8
), and (
C.4.2
), we conclude that if

N\gtrsim|\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}|+\log(1/\delta),

then with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\|(B^{t^{\prime},t})_{\cdot,\lambda^{\prime}}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|E_{\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}\mathbf{D}^{-(t^{\prime}-r_{1})}_{\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}\big(\widehat{\bar{\Sigma}}_{\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}},\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}\big)^{-1}\widehat{\bar{\Sigma}}_{\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}},\Omega^{c}_{\lambda^{\prime}}}\big(\mathbf{D}^{-r_{1}}_{\Omega^{c}_{\lambda^{\prime}}}\mathbf{A}_{\Omega^{c}_{\lambda^{\prime}},\lambda^{\prime}}\big)(\mathbf{D}_{\Lambda_{J}}^{-t})_{\lambda^{\prime},\lambda^{\prime}}}}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\|E_{\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}\|\cdot\|\mathbf{D}^{-(t^{\prime}-r_{1})}_{\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}\|\cdot\|\big(\widehat{\bar{\Sigma}}_{\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}},\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}\big)^{-1}\|\cdot\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|\widehat{\bar{\Sigma}}_{\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}},\Omega^{c}_{\lambda^{\prime}}}\big(\mathbf{D}^{-r_{1}}_{\Omega^{c}_{\lambda^{\prime}}}\mathbf{A}_{\Omega^{c}_{\lambda^{\prime}},\lambda^{\prime}}\big)(\mathbf{D}_{\Lambda_{J}}^{-t})_{\lambda^{\prime},\lambda^{\prime}}}}\right\|_{2}

(C.11)

\displaystyle\lesssim 2^{\widetilde{J}\max\{r_{1}-t^{\prime},0\}}\cdot\|\big(\mathbf{D}^{-r_{1}}_{\Omega^{c}_{\lambda^{\prime}}}\mathbf{A}_{\Omega^{c}_{\lambda^{\prime}},\lambda^{\prime}}\big)(\mathbf{D}_{\Lambda_{J}}^{-t})_{\lambda^{\prime},\lambda^{\prime}}\|_{2},

where the last inequality uses
\|E_{\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}\|=1,\|\mathbf{D}^{-(t^{\prime}-r_{1})}_{\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}\|\leq 2^{\widetilde{J}\max\{r_{1}-t^{\prime},0\}}
.

Let
\mathbf{A}^{\varepsilon}_{\Lambda_{\widetilde{J}}}
be the compressed matrix as in Definition
4.2
with parameters
(\widetilde{J},\,\widetilde{t},\,\widetilde{t}^{\prime})
, i.e.

\mathbf{A}^{\varepsilon}_{\Lambda_{\widetilde{J}}}:=M_{(\widetilde{J},\,\widetilde{t},\,\widetilde{t}^{\prime})}\odot\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{\widetilde{J}}}\,.

Applying Proposition
5.10
with parameters
(\widetilde{J},\,\widetilde{t},\,\widetilde{t}^{\prime})
, we obtain that, for
r_{1},t\in[r/2,\gamma)
,

\|\mathbf{D}^{-r_{1}}_{\Lambda_{\widetilde{J}}}(\mathbf{A}^{\varepsilon}_{\Lambda_{\widetilde{J}}}-\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{\widetilde{J}}})\mathbf{D}^{-t}_{\Lambda_{\widetilde{J}}}\|\lesssim\widetilde{J}2^{-\widetilde{J}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\min\{\widetilde{t}^{\prime},r_{1}\}+\min\{\widetilde{t},t\}-r}}\right)}=\widetilde{J}2^{-\widetilde{J}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(r_{1}+t-r}}\right)},

where the last equality follows since
\widetilde{t}=\max\{t,t^{\prime}\}\geq t
and
\widetilde{t}^{\prime}=\max\{r_{1},t^{\prime}\}\geq r_{1}
.

On the other hand, since
t>r/2
and
r_{1}>n/2+\max\{0,r\}\geq r/2
by Assumption
2.1
(ii), by Proposition
5.9
,

\|\mathbf{D}^{-r_{1}}(\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{\widetilde{J}}}^{\uparrow}-\mathbf{A})\mathbf{D}^{-t}\|\lesssim 2^{-\widetilde{J}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(r_{1}+t-r}}\right)}.

Recall that the regression support is defined by

\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}:=\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\{\lambda\in\mathcal{J}:(\lambda,\lambda^{\prime})\in\mathrm{supp}(\widetilde{J},\widetilde{t},\widetilde{t}^{\prime})}}\right\},\qquad\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}^{c}:=\mathcal{J}\setminus\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}.

For every
\lambda^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{J}
, the vector
\mathbf{A}_{\Omega^{c}_{\lambda^{\prime}},\lambda^{\prime}}
collects exactly those entries of the
\lambda^{\prime}
-th column of
\mathbf{A}
that are omitted from the regression. By construction of the truncation
\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{\widetilde{J}}}^{\uparrow}
and the compression mask
M_{(\widetilde{J},\,\widetilde{t},\,\widetilde{t}^{\prime})}
, these omitted entries coincide with the entries of the
\lambda^{\prime}
-th column of
\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{\widetilde{J}}}^{\uparrow}-\mathbf{A}
and
\mathbf{A}^{\varepsilon}_{\Lambda_{\widetilde{J}}}-\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{\widetilde{J}}}
. Therefore, after weighting by the diagonal matrices on both sides,

(C.12)

\displaystyle\|\big(\mathbf{D}^{-r_{1}}_{\Omega^{c}_{\lambda^{\prime}}}\mathbf{A}_{\Omega^{c}_{\lambda^{\prime}},\lambda^{\prime}}\big)(\mathbf{D}_{\Lambda_{J}}^{-t})_{\lambda^{\prime},\lambda^{\prime}}\|_{2}\leq\|\mathbf{D}^{-r_{1}}(\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{\widetilde{J}}}^{\uparrow}-\mathbf{A})\mathbf{D}^{-t}\|+\|\mathbf{D}^{-r_{1}}_{\Lambda_{\widetilde{J}}}(\mathbf{A}^{\varepsilon}_{\Lambda_{\widetilde{J}}}-\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{\widetilde{J}}})\mathbf{D}^{-t}_{\Lambda_{\widetilde{J}}}\|\lesssim\widetilde{J}2^{-\widetilde{J}(r_{1}+t-r)}.

Combining (
C.4.2
) and (
C.12
) yields

\displaystyle\|(B^{t^{\prime},t})_{\cdot,\lambda^{\prime}}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim 2^{\widetilde{J}\max\{r_{1}-t^{\prime},0\}}\cdot\|\big(\mathbf{D}^{-r_{1}}_{\Omega^{c}_{\lambda^{\prime}}}\mathbf{A}_{\Omega^{c}_{\lambda^{\prime}},\lambda^{\prime}}\big)(\mathbf{D}_{\Lambda_{J}}^{-t})_{\lambda^{\prime},\lambda^{\prime}}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim 2^{\widetilde{J}\max\{r_{1}-t^{\prime},0\}}\cdot\widetilde{J}2^{-\widetilde{J}(r_{1}+t-r)}

\displaystyle=\widetilde{J}2^{-\widetilde{J}(\min\{t^{\prime},r_{1}\}+t-r)}.

Taking a union bound over all columns indexed by
\lambda^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{J}
, we obtain that, if

N\gtrsim\max_{\lambda^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{J}}|\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}|+J+\log(1/\delta),

then with probability at least
1-\delta
, the following bound holds simultaneously for all
\lambda^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{J}
:

\|(B^{t^{\prime},t})_{\cdot,\lambda^{\prime}}\|_{2}\lesssim\widetilde{J}2^{-\widetilde{J}(\min\{t^{\prime},r_{1}\}+t-r)}.

For any matrix
B
, write
\mathrm{nnz}(B):=\#\{(i,j):B_{ij}\neq 0\}
,
\mathrm{nnz}_{\mathrm{row}}(B):=\max_{i}\#\{j:B_{ij}\neq 0\}
, and
\mathrm{nnz}_{\mathrm{col}}(B):=\max_{j}\#\{i:B_{ij}\neq 0\}
. By Proposition
C.2
,

\mathrm{nnz}_{\mathrm{row}}(\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})\lesssim 2^{Jn(t+t^{\prime}-r)/(\sigma-\frac{n}{2}+t-\frac{r}{2})},\quad\mathrm{nnz}_{\mathrm{col}}(\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})\lesssim 2^{Jn(t+t^{\prime}-r)/(\sigma-\frac{n}{2}+t^{\prime}-\frac{r}{2})}.

Hence,

\displaystyle\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|(\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{\mathrm{up}}\odot(B^{t^{\prime},t})_{\Lambda_{J},\cdot}}}\right\|_{1}

\displaystyle=\max_{\lambda^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{J}}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left((\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{\mathrm{up}}\odot(B^{t^{\prime},t})_{\Lambda_{J},\cdot}}}\right)_{\cdot,\lambda^{\prime}}}}\right\|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{\lambda^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{J}}\sqrt{\mathrm{nnz}_{\mathrm{col}}((\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{\mathrm{up}})}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left((\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{\mathrm{up}}\odot(B^{t^{\prime},t})_{\Lambda_{J},\cdot}}}\right)_{\cdot,\lambda^{\prime}}}}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{\lambda^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{J}}\sqrt{\mathrm{nnz}_{\mathrm{col}}(\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})}\cdot\,\|(B^{t^{\prime},t})_{\cdot,\lambda^{\prime}}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim 2^{Jn(t+t^{\prime}-r)/2(\sigma-\frac{n}{2}+t^{\prime}-\frac{r}{2})}\cdot\widetilde{J}2^{-\widetilde{J}(\min\{t^{\prime},r_{1}\}+t-r)}.

On the other hand,

\displaystyle\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|(\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{\mathrm{up}}\odot(B^{t^{\prime},t})_{\Lambda_{J},\cdot}}}\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle=\max_{\lambda\in\Lambda_{J}}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left((\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{\mathrm{up}}\odot(B^{t^{\prime},t})_{\Lambda_{J},\cdot}}}\right)_{\lambda,\cdot}}}\right\|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\mathrm{nnz}_{\mathrm{row}}((\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{\mathrm{up}})\cdot\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|(\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{\mathrm{up}}\odot(B^{t^{\prime},t})_{\Lambda_{J},\cdot}}}\right\|_{\max}

\displaystyle\leq\mathrm{nnz}_{\mathrm{row}}(\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})\cdot\max_{\lambda^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{J}}\|(B^{t^{\prime},t})_{\cdot,\lambda^{\prime}}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim 2^{Jn(t+t^{\prime}-r)/(\sigma-\frac{n}{2}+t-\frac{r}{2})}\cdot\widetilde{J}2^{-\widetilde{J}(\min\{t^{\prime},r_{1}\}+t-r)}.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|(\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{\mathrm{up}}\odot(B^{t^{\prime},t})_{\Lambda_{J},\cdot}}}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\,\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|(\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{\mathrm{up}}\odot(B^{t^{\prime},t})_{\Lambda_{J},\cdot}}}\right\|_{1}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|(\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{\mathrm{up}}\odot(B^{t^{\prime},t})_{\Lambda_{J},\cdot}}}\right\|_{\infty}}}\right)^{1/2}

(C.13)

\displaystyle\lesssim\widetilde{J}2^{-\widetilde{J}(\min\{t^{\prime},r_{1}\}+t-r)}\cdot 2^{\varepsilon_{1}J},

where

\varepsilon_{1}:=\frac{n(t+t^{\prime}-r)}{\sigma-n/2+t-r/2}\geq n(t+t^{\prime}-r)\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\frac{1}{4(\sigma-n/2+t^{\prime}-r/2)}+\frac{1}{2(\sigma-n/2+t-r/2)}}}\right).

Similarly, if
N\gtrsim\max_{\lambda^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{J}}|\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}|+J+\log(1/\delta)
, then with probability at least
1-\delta
, the following bound holds simultaneously for all
\lambda^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{J}
:

\|(B^{t,t^{\prime}})_{\cdot,\lambda^{\prime}}\|\lesssim\widetilde{J}2^{-\widetilde{J}(\min\{t,r_{1}\}+t^{\prime}-r)}.

Using the same argument yields

(C.14)

\displaystyle\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|((\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{\mathrm{low}})^{\top}\odot(B^{t,t^{\prime}})_{\Lambda_{J},\cdot}}}\right\|\lesssim\widetilde{J}2^{-\widetilde{J}(\min\{t,r_{1}\}+t^{\prime}-r)}\cdot 2^{\varepsilon_{1}J},

where we used

\varepsilon_{1}=\frac{n(t+t^{\prime}-r)}{\sigma-n/2+t-r/2}\geq n(t+t^{\prime}-r)\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\frac{1}{4(\sigma-n/2+t-r/2)}+\frac{1}{2(\sigma-n/2+t^{\prime}-r/2)}}}\right).

Combining (
C.6
), (
C.4.2
), and (
C.14
) yields that, if
N\gtrsim\max_{\lambda^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{J}}|\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}|+J+\log(1/\delta)
, then with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\mathsf{Error\text{-}OVB}

\displaystyle=\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|(\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{\mathrm{up}}\odot(B^{t^{\prime},t})_{\Lambda_{J},\cdot}}}\right\|+\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|((\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{\mathrm{low}})^{\top}\odot(B^{t,t^{\prime}})_{\Lambda_{J},\cdot}}}\right\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\widetilde{J}2^{-\widetilde{J}(\min\{t^{\prime},r_{1}\}+t-r)}\cdot 2^{\varepsilon_{1}J}+\widetilde{J}2^{-\widetilde{J}(\min\{t,r_{1}\}+t^{\prime}-r)}\cdot 2^{\varepsilon_{1}J}

\displaystyle\lesssim J\,2^{-J(t+t^{\prime}-r)},

where the last line follows from the definition of
\widetilde{J}

\widetilde{J}=\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\lceil\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r+\varepsilon_{1}}{\min\{t^{\prime},r_{1}\}+t-r}\,J}}\right\rceil\geq\max\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\{\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r+\varepsilon_{1}}{\min\{t^{\prime},r_{1}\}+t-r}J,\,\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r+\varepsilon_{1}}{\min\{t,r_{1}\}+t^{\prime}-r}J}}\right\}.

The final equality holds because
t\leq t^{\prime}
implies

\min\{t^{\prime},r_{1}\}+t-r\leq\min\{t,r_{1}\}+t^{\prime}-r.

In summary, we use the set
\mathrm{supp}(\widetilde{J},\widetilde{t},\widetilde{t}^{\prime})
to perform the regression. Then, we conclude that, if

N\gtrsim\max_{\lambda^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{J}}|\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}|+J+\log(1/\delta)\asymp 2^{\widetilde{J}n(\widetilde{t}+\widetilde{t}^{\prime}-r)/(\sigma-\frac{n}{2}+\widetilde{t}^{\prime}-\frac{r}{2})}+\log(1/\delta),

then with probability at least
1-\delta
,

(C.15)

\displaystyle\mathsf{Error\text{-}OVB}\lesssim J\,2^{-J(t+t^{\prime}-r)}.

∎

C.4.3
Variance

Proof of Proposition
5.12
.

Recall that
