\displaystyle\mathsf{Error\text{-}Var}

\displaystyle=\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|\mathbf{D}^{-t^{\prime}}_{\Lambda_{J}}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left((\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{\mathrm{up}}\odot\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\mathsf{Var}_{\Lambda_{J},\cdot}}}\right)}}\right)\mathbf{D}^{-t}_{\Lambda_{J}}}}\right\|+\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|\mathbf{D}^{-t}_{\Lambda_{J}}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(((\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{\mathrm{low}})^{\top}\odot\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\mathsf{Var}_{\Lambda_{J},\cdot}}}\right)}}\right)\mathbf{D}^{-t^{\prime}}_{\Lambda_{J}}}}\right\|

\displaystyle=\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|(\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{\mathrm{up}}\odot\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\mathbf{D}^{-t^{\prime}}_{\Lambda_{J}}\mathsf{Var}_{\Lambda_{J},\cdot}\mathbf{D}^{-t}_{\Lambda_{J}}}}\right)}}\right\|+\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|((\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{\mathrm{low}})^{\top}\odot\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\mathbf{D}^{-t}_{\Lambda_{J}}\mathsf{Var}_{\Lambda_{J},\cdot}\mathbf{D}^{-t^{\prime}}_{\Lambda_{J}}}}\right)}}\right\|,

where

\mathsf{Var}:=\sum_{\lambda^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{J}}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(E_{\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}(\mathbf{U}_{\cdot,\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{\cdot,\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}})^{-1}\mathbf{U}_{\cdot,\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{\cdot,\lambda^{\prime}}}}\right)e_{\lambda^{\prime}}^{\top}\in\mathbb{R}^{\Lambda_{\widetilde{J}}\,\times\Lambda_{J}}.

Define
V^{t^{\prime},t}:=\mathbf{D}_{\Lambda_{\widetilde{J}}}^{-t^{\prime}}\,\mathsf{Var}\,\mathbf{D}_{\Lambda_{J}}^{-t}
and
V^{t,t^{\prime}}:=\mathbf{D}_{\Lambda_{\widetilde{J}}}^{-t}\,\mathsf{Var}\,\mathbf{D}_{\Lambda_{J}}^{-t^{\prime}}
, then

(C.16)

\displaystyle\mathsf{Error\text{-}Var}=\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|(\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{\mathrm{up}}\odot(V^{t^{\prime},t})_{\Lambda_{J},\cdot}}}\right\|+\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|((\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{\mathrm{low}})^{\top}\odot(V^{t,t^{\prime}})_{\Lambda_{J},\cdot}}}\right\|.

To control the first term in (
C.16
), we apply the norm-compression inequality (Lemma
C.3
) with the natural
(j,j^{\prime})
-block partition, writing
(\cdot)_{j,j^{\prime}}
for the
(j,j^{\prime})
-block. We obtain that

\displaystyle\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|(\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{\mathrm{up}}\odot(V^{t^{\prime},t})_{\Lambda_{J},\cdot}}}\right\|\leq\bigg(\sum_{j_{0}\leq j\leq j^{\prime}\leq J}\bigg\|\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{\mathrm{up}}\odot(V^{t^{\prime},t})_{\Lambda_{J},\cdot}}}\right)_{j,j^{\prime}}\bigg\|^{2}\bigg)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq\bigg(\sum_{j_{0}\leq j\leq j^{\prime}\leq J}\bigg\|\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{\mathrm{up}}\odot(V^{t^{\prime},t})_{\Lambda_{J},\cdot}}}\right)_{j,j^{\prime}}\bigg\|_{1}\cdot\bigg\|\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{\mathrm{up}}\odot(V^{t^{\prime},t})_{\Lambda_{J},\cdot}}}\right)_{j,j^{\prime}}\bigg\|_{\infty}\bigg)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq\bigg(\sum_{j_{0}\leq j\leq j^{\prime}\leq J}\mathrm{nnz}_{\mathrm{col}}\big(\big(\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})}\big)_{j,j^{\prime}}\big)\cdot\mathrm{nnz}_{\mathrm{row}}\big(\big(\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})}\big)_{j,j^{\prime}}\big)\cdot\big\|\big(V^{t^{\prime},t}\big)_{j,j^{\prime}}\big\|^{2}_{\max}\bigg)^{1/2}

(C.17)

\displaystyle=\bigg(\sum_{j_{0}\leq j\leq j^{\prime}\leq J}2^{-2jt^{\prime}-2j^{\prime}t}\cdot(\mathrm{count}(j,j^{\prime}))^{2}\cdot\|(\mathsf{Var})_{j,j^{\prime}}\|^{2}_{\max}\bigg)^{1/2},

where in the last line we used
(V^{t^{\prime},t})_{j,j^{\prime}}=2^{-jt^{\prime}-j^{\prime}t}(\mathsf{Var})_{j,j^{\prime}}
and

\mathrm{count}(j,j^{\prime}):=\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\mathrm{nnz}_{\mathrm{col}}\big(\big(\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})}\big)_{j,j^{\prime}}\big)\cdot\mathrm{nnz}_{\mathrm{row}}\big(\big(\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})}\big)_{j,j^{\prime}}\big)}}\right)^{1/2}.

By Proposition
C.2
, we have

(C.18)

\displaystyle\mathrm{count}(j,j^{\prime})\lesssim\begin{cases}0&\text{if }(j,j^{\prime})\in D_{1}\cup D_{2},\\
2^{(j-j^{\prime})n/2}&\text{if }(j,j^{\prime})\in D_{3},\\
2^{(j^{\prime}-j)n/2}&\text{if }(j,j^{\prime})\in D_{4},\\
2^{(j+j^{\prime})n/2}2^{n(J(t+t^{\prime}-r)-jt^{\prime}-j^{\prime}t-(j+j^{\prime})\widetilde{d})/(2\widetilde{d}+r)}&\text{if }(j,j^{\prime})\in D_{5},\\
2^{(j+j^{\prime})n/2}&\text{if }(j,j^{\prime})\in D_{6}.\end{cases}

Next, we derive a high-probability upper bound on
\|(\mathsf{Var})_{j,j^{\prime}}\|_{\max}
for all
j_{0}\leq j,j^{\prime}\leq J
.

For
j_{0}\leq j,j^{\prime}\leq J
and
\lambda^{\prime}=(j^{\prime},k^{\prime})
with
k^{\prime}\in\nabla_{j^{\prime}}
, let
(\mathsf{Var})_{j,\lambda^{\prime}}=(\mathsf{Var})_{j,(j^{\prime},k^{\prime})}\in\mathbb{R}^{\nabla_{j}}
denote the
\lambda^{\prime}
-th column of
(\mathsf{Var})_{j,j^{\prime}}
. Then,

\|(\mathsf{Var})_{j,j^{\prime}}\|_{\max}=\max_{k^{\prime}\in\nabla_{j^{\prime}}}\|(\mathsf{Var})_{j,\lambda^{\prime}}\|_{\max}.

Recall that the
\lambda^{\prime}
-th column of the variance term
\mathsf{Var}
is given by

(\mathsf{Var})_{\cdot,\lambda^{\prime}}=E_{\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}(\mathbf{U}_{\cdot,\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{\cdot,\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}})^{-1}\mathbf{U}_{\cdot,\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{\cdot,\lambda^{\prime}}\in\mathbb{R}^{\Lambda_{\widetilde{J}}},

where
\mathbf{W}_{\cdot,\lambda^{\prime}}\sim\mathcal{N}(0,\sigma^{2}_{\lambda^{\prime}}I_{N})
is independent of
\mathbf{U}
, and
\sigma_{\lambda^{\prime}}\asymp 2^{-j^{\prime}r_{2}}
by Proposition
3.2

(II)
(iii). Conditioning on
\mathbf{U}
,

(\mathbf{U}_{\cdot,\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{\cdot,\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}})^{-1}\mathbf{U}_{\cdot,\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{\cdot,\lambda^{\prime}}\,\mid\,\mathbf{U}\sim\mathcal{N}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(0,\sigma^{2}_{\lambda^{\prime}}(\mathbf{U}_{\cdot,\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{\cdot,\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}})^{-1}}}\right).

Write
\widehat{\Sigma}_{\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}},\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}:=\frac{1}{N}\mathbf{U}_{\cdot,\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{\cdot,\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}
and
\widehat{\bar{\Sigma}}_{\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}},\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}:=\mathbf{D}^{r_{1}}_{\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}\widehat{\Sigma}_{\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}},\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}\mathbf{D}^{r_{1}}_{\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}
. Hence,

(\mathbf{U}_{\cdot,\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{\cdot,\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}})^{-1}\mathbf{U}_{\cdot,\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{\cdot,\lambda^{\prime}}\,\mid\,\mathbf{U}\sim\mathcal{N}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(0,\frac{\sigma^{2}_{\lambda^{\prime}}}{N}\,\widehat{\Sigma}_{\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}},\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}^{-1}}}\right)=\mathcal{N}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(0,\frac{\sigma^{2}_{\lambda^{\prime}}}{N}\,\mathbf{D}^{r_{1}}_{\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}\widehat{\bar{\Sigma}}_{\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}},\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}^{-1}\mathbf{D}^{r_{1}}_{\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}}}\right).

Observe that the vector
(\mathsf{Var})_{j,\lambda^{\prime}}\in\mathbb{R}^{\nabla_{j}}
is the subvector of
(\mathsf{Var})_{\cdot,\lambda^{\prime}}
obtained by restricting the row index to the
j
-th block. Therefore, conditioning on
\mathbf{U}
, the nonzero entries in
(\mathsf{Var})_{j,\lambda^{\prime}}
has the same distribution as
v_{j,\lambda^{\prime}}
, where

v_{j,\lambda^{\prime}}\sim\mathcal{N}\bigg(0,\frac{2^{2jr_{1}}\sigma^{2}_{\lambda^{\prime}}}{N}\big(\widehat{\bar{\Sigma}}_{\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}},\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}^{-1}\big)_{j,j}\bigg).

Let
\bar{v}_{j,\lambda^{\prime}}:=v_{j,\lambda^{\prime}}\big(\frac{2^{jr_{1}}\sigma_{\lambda^{\prime}}}{\sqrt{N}}\big)^{-1}
, then
\bar{v}_{j,\lambda^{\prime}}\sim\mathcal{N}\big(0,\big(\widehat{\bar{\Sigma}}_{\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}},\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}^{-1}\big)_{j,j}\big)
.

Fix
\delta\in(0,1)
and set

\delta_{j,\lambda^{\prime}}:=2^{-j^{\prime}n}J^{-2}\delta,\quad\lambda^{\prime}=(j^{\prime},k^{\prime}).

Since
\bar{\Sigma}_{\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}},\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}}
is well-conditioned with eigenvalues of order one, Lemma
C.5
implies that, provided
N\gtrsim|\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}|+\log(1/\delta_{j,\lambda^{\prime}})
, we have with probability at least
1-\delta_{j,\lambda^{\prime}}
,

\|\bar{v}_{j,\lambda^{\prime}}\|_{\max}\lesssim\sqrt{\log\big(\mathrm{dim}(\bar{v}_{j,\lambda^{\prime}})/\delta_{j,\lambda^{\prime}}\big)}\lesssim\sqrt{\log\big(2^{jn}/\delta_{j,\lambda^{\prime}}\big)}\lesssim\sqrt{j}+\sqrt{\log(1/\delta_{j,\lambda^{\prime}})}.

Taking a union bound over
k^{\prime}\in\nabla_{j^{\prime}}
, if
N\gtrsim\max_{k^{\prime}\in\nabla_{j^{\prime}}}\big(|\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}|+\log(1/\delta_{j,\lambda^{\prime}})\big)
, then with probability at least
1-\sum_{k^{\prime}\in\nabla_{j^{\prime}}}\delta_{j,\lambda^{\prime}}
,

\max_{k^{\prime}\in\nabla_{j^{\prime}}}\|\bar{v}_{j,\lambda^{\prime}}\|_{\max}\lesssim\sqrt{j}+\sqrt{\log(1/\min_{k^{\prime}\in\nabla_{j^{\prime}}}\delta_{j,\lambda^{\prime}})}.

Finally, applying a union bound over all
(j,j^{\prime})
–blocks, if

N\gtrsim\max_{j,j^{\prime}}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\max_{k^{\prime}\in\nabla_{j^{\prime}}}\big(|\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}|+\log(1/\delta_{j,\lambda^{\prime}})\big)}}\right)\asymp\max_{\lambda^{\prime}}|\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}|+J+\log(1/\delta),

then with probability at least
1-\sum_{j,j^{\prime}}\sum_{k^{\prime}\in\nabla_{j^{\prime}}}\delta_{j,\lambda^{\prime}}\geq 1-\delta
, we have simultaneously for all
j,j^{\prime}
,

\max_{k^{\prime}\in\nabla_{j^{\prime}}}\|\bar{v}_{j,\lambda^{\prime}}\|_{\max}\lesssim\sqrt{j}+\sqrt{\log(1/\min_{k^{\prime}\in\nabla_{j^{\prime}}}\delta_{j,\lambda^{\prime}})}\lesssim\sqrt{J+\log(1/\delta)}.

Recall that
\max_{\lambda^{\prime}}|\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}|
denotes the maximal column-wise regression support size over
\lambda^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{J}
. Since the regression support is chosen as
\mathrm{supp}(\widetilde{J},\widetilde{t},\widetilde{t}^{\prime})
defined in (
4.2
), we have

\max_{\lambda^{\prime}}|\Omega_{\lambda^{\prime}}|=\mathrm{nnz}_{\mathrm{col}}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\mathrm{supp}(\widetilde{J},\widetilde{t},\widetilde{t}^{\prime})}}\right)\lesssim 2^{\widetilde{J}n(\widetilde{t}+\widetilde{t}^{\prime}-r)/(\sigma-\frac{n}{2}+\widetilde{t}^{\prime}-\frac{r}{2})},

as stated in Proposition
C.2
.

Consequently, if

N\gtrsim 2^{\widetilde{J}n(\widetilde{t}+\widetilde{t}^{\prime}-r)/(\sigma-n/2+\widetilde{t}^{\prime}-r/2)}+\log(1/\delta),

then with probability at least
1-\delta
, simultaneously for all
j,j^{\prime}
,

\displaystyle\|(\mathsf{Var})_{j,j^{\prime}}\|_{\max}

\displaystyle=\max_{k^{\prime}\in\nabla_{j^{\prime}}}\|(\mathsf{Var})_{j,\lambda^{\prime}}\|_{\max}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{k^{\prime}\in\nabla_{j^{\prime}}}\frac{2^{jr_{1}}\sigma_{\lambda^{\prime}}}{\sqrt{N}}\|\bar{v}_{j,\lambda^{\prime}}\|_{\max}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{2^{jr_{1}-j^{\prime}r_{2}}}{\sqrt{N}}\max_{k^{\prime}\in\nabla_{j^{\prime}}}\|\bar{v}_{j,\lambda^{\prime}}\|_{\max}

(C.19)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{2^{jr_{1}-j^{\prime}r_{2}}}{\sqrt{N}}\cdot\sqrt{J+\log(1/\delta)}.

Let
D_{\mathrm{up}}:=\{(j,j^{\prime}):0\leq j\leq j^{\prime}\leq J\}
and
D_{\mathrm{low}}:=\{(j,j^{\prime}):0\leq j^{\prime}<j\leq J\}
. Combining the estimates (
C.4.3
), (
C.18
), and (
C.4.3
) yields that, if
N\gtrsim 2^{\widetilde{J}n(\widetilde{t}+\widetilde{t}^{\prime}-r)/(\sigma-n/2+\widetilde{t}^{\prime}-r/2)}+\log(1/\delta)
, then with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|(\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{\mathrm{up}}\odot(V^{t^{\prime},t})_{\Lambda_{J},\cdot}}}\right\|\leq\bigg(\sum_{j_{0}\leq j\leq j^{\prime}\leq J}2^{-2jt^{\prime}-2j^{\prime}t}\cdot(\mathrm{count}(j,j^{\prime}))^{2}\cdot\|(\mathsf{Var})_{j,j^{\prime}}\|^{2}_{\max}\bigg)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\frac{J+\log(1/\delta)}{N}}\bigg(\sum_{(j,j^{\prime})\in D_{\mathrm{up}}}2^{2j(r_{1}-t^{\prime})-2j^{\prime}(r_{2}+t)}\cdot(\mathrm{count}(j,j^{\prime}))^{2}\bigg)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{J+\log(1/\delta)}{N}}\bigg(\sum_{(j,j^{\prime})\in D_{4}}2^{2j(r_{1}-t^{\prime})-2j^{\prime}(r_{2}+t)}\cdot 2^{(j^{\prime}-j)n}+\sum_{(j,j^{\prime})\in D_{\mathrm{up}}\cap D_{6}}2^{2j(r_{1}-t^{\prime})-2j^{\prime}(r_{2}+t)}\cdot 2^{(j+j^{\prime})n}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{(j,j^{\prime})\in D_{\mathrm{up}}\cap D_{5}}2^{2j(r_{1}-t^{\prime})-2j^{\prime}(r_{2}+t)}\cdot 2^{(j+j^{\prime})n}2^{2n(J(t+t^{\prime}-r)-jt^{\prime}-j^{\prime}t-(j+j^{\prime})\widetilde{d})/(2\widetilde{d}+r)}\bigg)^{1/2}

(C.20)

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{J+\log(1/\delta)}{N}}\cdot J\cdot\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(1+2^{J\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\sigma-n/2+t-r/2}(-t-r_{2}+n/2)}+2^{J\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{2\widetilde{d}+t+t^{\prime}}(-t-t^{\prime}+r_{1}-r_{2}+n)}+2^{J(-t-t^{\prime}+r_{1}-r_{2})}}}\right),

where, in the last step, we repeatedly used the standard exponential–sum estimate

\sum_{j=k_{1}}^{k_{2}}2^{j\alpha}\asymp\begin{cases}2^{k_{2}\alpha}&\text{ if }\alpha>0,\\
k_{2}-k_{1}&\text{ if }\alpha=0,\\
2^{k_{1}}\alpha&\text{ if }\alpha<0,\\
\end{cases}

and, moreover, since the regions
D_{1}
-
D_{6}
are delineated by straight lines, the dominant contributions to the sum arise from the pairs
(j,j^{\prime})
lying on the boundary lines and at the corner points. This observation reduces the extraction of the leading-order terms to checking finitely many boundary/corner contributions. Indeed, the four terms inside the parentheses in the last line of (
C.4.3
) correspond to the contributions from the following four blocks in the
(j,j^{\prime})
–plane:

(0,0),\quad\bigg(0,\ \frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}+t-\frac{r}{2}}J\bigg),\quad\bigg(\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{2\widetilde{d}+t+t^{\prime}}J,\ \frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{2\widetilde{d}+t+t^{\prime}}J\bigg),\quad(J,J).

Similarly, for the second term in (
C.16
), we have

\displaystyle\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|((\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{\mathrm{low}})^{\top}\odot(V^{t,t^{\prime}})_{\Lambda_{J},\cdot}}}\right\|=\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|(\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{\mathrm{low}}\odot(V^{t,t^{\prime}})^{\top}_{\Lambda_{J},\cdot}}}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\bigg(\sum_{j_{0}\leq j^{\prime}<j\leq J}2^{-2jt^{\prime}-2j^{\prime}t}\cdot(\mathrm{count}(j,j^{\prime}))^{2}\cdot\|(\mathsf{Var})_{j^{\prime},j}\|^{2}_{\max}\bigg)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{J+\log(1/\delta)}{N}}\bigg(\sum_{(j,j^{\prime})\in D_{\mathrm{low}}}2^{2j^{\prime}(r_{1}-t)-2j(r_{2}+t^{\prime})}\cdot(\mathrm{count}(j,j^{\prime}))^{2}\bigg)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{J+\log(1/\delta)}{N}}\bigg(\sum_{(j,j^{\prime})\in D_{3}}2^{2j^{\prime}(r_{1}-t)-2j(r_{2}+t^{\prime})}\cdot 2^{(j-j^{\prime})n}+\sum_{(j,j^{\prime})\in D_{\mathrm{low}}\cap D_{6}}2^{2j^{\prime}(r_{1}-t)-2j(r_{2}+t^{\prime})}\cdot 2^{(j+j^{\prime})n}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{(j,j^{\prime})\in D_{\mathrm{low}}\cap D_{5}}2^{2j^{\prime}(r_{1}-t)-2j(r_{2}+t^{\prime})}\cdot 2^{(j+j^{\prime})n}2^{2n(J(t+t^{\prime}-r)-jt^{\prime}-j^{\prime}t-(j+j^{\prime})\widetilde{d})/(2\widetilde{d}+r)}\bigg)^{1/2}

(C.21)

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{J+\log(1/\delta)}{N}}\cdot J\cdot\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(1+2^{J\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\sigma-n/2+t^{\prime}-r/2}(-t^{\prime}-r_{2}+n/2)}+2^{J\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{2\widetilde{d}+t+t^{\prime}}(-t-t^{\prime}+r_{1}-r_{2}+n)}+2^{J(-t-t^{\prime}+r_{1}-r_{2})}}}\right).

Therefore, combining (
C.16
), (
C.4.3
), and (
C.4.3
), we conclude that, if

N\gtrsim 2^{\widetilde{J}n(\widetilde{t}+\widetilde{t}^{\prime}-r)/(\sigma-n/2+\widetilde{t}^{\prime}-r/2)}+\log(1/\delta),

then with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\mathsf{Error\text{-}Var}=\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|(\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{\mathrm{up}}\odot(V^{t^{\prime},t})_{\Lambda_{J},\cdot}}}\right\|+\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|((\mathbf{M}_{(J,t,t^{\prime})})_{\mathrm{low}})^{\top}\odot(V^{t,t^{\prime}})_{\Lambda_{J},\cdot}}}\right\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{J+\log(1/\delta)}{N}}\cdot J\cdot\Bigg(1+2^{J\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\sigma-n/2+t^{\prime}-r/2}(-t^{\prime}-r_{2}+n/2)}+2^{J\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\sigma-n/2+t-r/2}(-t-r_{2}+n/2)}

\displaystyle\quad+2^{J\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{2\widetilde{d}+t+t^{\prime}}(-t-t^{\prime}+r_{1}-r_{2}+n)}+2^{J(-t-t^{\prime}+r_{1}-r_{2})}\Bigg)

(C.22)

\displaystyle\asymp\sqrt{\frac{J+\log(1/\delta)}{N}}\cdot J\cdot 2^{\rho J(t+t^{\prime}-r)/2},

where

\rho=2\max\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\{\frac{-t-r_{2}+n/2}{\sigma-n/2+t-r/2},\,\frac{-t^{\prime}-r_{2}+n/2}{\sigma-n/2+t^{\prime}-r/2},\,\frac{-t-t^{\prime}+r_{1}-r_{2}+n}{t+t^{\prime}+2\widetilde{d}},\,\frac{-t-t^{\prime}+r_{1}-r_{2}}{t+t^{\prime}-r},0}}\right\}.

The leading contributions to the final variance arise from the following five blocks in the
(j,j^{\prime})
–plane:

(0,0),\ \ \bigg(0,\ \frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}+t-\frac{r}{2}}J\bigg),\ \ \bigg(\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{\sigma-\frac{n}{2}+t^{\prime}-\frac{r}{2}}J,\ 0\bigg),\ \ \bigg(\frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{2\widetilde{d}+t+t^{\prime}}J,\ \frac{t+t^{\prime}-r}{2\widetilde{d}+t+t^{\prime}}J\bigg),\ \ (J,J).

∎

C.5
Technical Lemmas

Lemma C.3

(Norm compression inequality)
.

Let
A\in\mathbb{R}^{p\times p}
and let
(I_{1},\dots,I_{G})
be a partition of
\{1,\dots,p\}
with
|I_{j}|=p_{j}
and
p_{1}+\cdots+p_{G}=p
.
For
1\leq i,j\leq G
write
A_{ij}:=A[I_{i},I_{j}]\in\mathbb{R}^{p_{i}\times p_{j}}
for the
(i,j)
block.
Define the
norm compression
of
A
to be the matrix

\mathcal{N}(A)\in\mathbb{R}^{G\times G},\qquad\big(\mathcal{N}(A)\big)_{ij}:=\|A_{ij}\|,

where
\|\cdot\|
denotes the operator norm. For any
A\in\mathbb{R}^{p\times p}
and any partition
(I_{1},\dots,I_{G})
as above,

\|A\|\leq\|\mathcal{N}(A)\|\leq\bigg(\sum_{i,j=1}^{G}\|A_{ij}\|^{2}\bigg)^{1/2}.

Proof of Lemma
C.3
.

Let
x\in\mathbb{R}^{p}
with
\|x\|_{2}=1
and decompose
x=(x_{1};\dots;x_{G})
with
x_{j}\in\mathbb{R}^{p_{j}}
.
Then the
i
th block of
Ax
is
\sum_{j=1}^{G}A_{ij}x_{j}
, so by the triangle inequality and the definition of the spectral norm,

\|(Ax)_{i}\|_{2}\;\leq\;\sum_{j=1}^{G}\|A_{ij}\|\,\|x_{j}\|_{2}\;=\;\sum_{j=1}^{G}\big(\mathcal{N}(A)\big)_{ij}\,y_{j},

where
y=(y_{1},\dots,y_{G})^{\top}
with
y_{j}:=\|x_{j}\|_{2}
.
Therefore,

\|Ax\|_{2}^{2}=\sum_{i=1}^{G}\|(Ax)_{i}\|_{2}^{2}\;\leq\;\sum_{i=1}^{G}\Big(\sum_{j=1}^{G}\big(\mathcal{N}(A)\big)_{ij}y_{j}\Big)^{2}=\|\mathcal{N}(A)\,y\|_{2}^{2}\;\leq\;\|\mathcal{N}(A)\|^{2}\,\|y\|_{2}^{2}\;\leq\;\|\mathcal{N}(A)\|^{2},

since
\|y\|_{2}^{2}=\sum_{j}\|x_{j}\|_{2}^{2}=\|x\|_{2}^{2}=1
.
Taking the supremum over all unit vectors
x
gives the first inequality. The second inequality in the claim follows from

\|\mathcal{N}(A)\|\leq\|\mathcal{N}(A)\|_{F}=\bigg(\sum_{i,j=1}^{G}\|A_{ij}\|^{2}\bigg)^{1/2}.

∎

Lemma C.4
.

Let
(X,Z)
be jointly mean-zero Gaussian with
X\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
and
Z\in\mathbb{R}
.
Write

\mu:=\mathbb{E}[XZ]\in\mathbb{R}^{d},\qquad\sigma_{Z}^{2}:=\mathbb{E}[Z^{2}],\qquad\Sigma_{XX}:=\mathbb{E}[XX^{\top}].

Let
(X_{i},Z_{i})_{i=1}^{N}
be i.i.d. copies of
(X,Z)
.
Then, for any
\delta\in(0,1)
, with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\bigg\|\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}X_{i}Z_{i}-\mu\bigg\|_{2}\leq C\big(\sigma_{Z}\|\Sigma_{XX}\|^{1/2}+\|\mu\|_{2}\big)\!\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\sqrt{\frac{d+\log(2/\delta)}{N}}+\frac{d+\log(2/\delta)}{N}}}\right),

for a universal constant
C>0
.

Proof of Lemma
C.4
.

Fix a unit vector
u\in\mathbb{S}^{d-1}
. Since both
\langle u,X\rangle
and
Z
are centered Gaussian, with

\|\langle u,X\rangle\|_{\psi_{2}}\lesssim\|\Sigma_{XX}\|^{1/2}
,

\|Z\|_{\psi_{2}}\lesssim\sigma_{Z}
, hence their product is sub-exponential
(see, e.g.,
[
139
, Lemma 2.7.7]
) and

\displaystyle\|\langle u,XZ-\mu\rangle\|_{\psi_{1}}\lesssim\|\langle u,X\rangle Z\|_{\psi_{1}}+|\langle u,\mu\rangle|\lesssim\sigma_{Z}\|\Sigma_{XX}\|^{1/2}+\|\mu\|_{2}.

Let
W_{i}(u):=\langle u,X_{i}Z_{i}-\mu\rangle
. By the scalar Bernstein inequality for independent sub-exponential variables
(
[
139
, Theorem 2.8.1]
), for any
t>0
,

(C.23)

\mathbb{P}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left[\bigg|\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}W_{i}(u)\bigg|\geq C(\sigma_{Z}\|\Sigma_{XX}\|^{1/2}+\|\mu\|_{2})\bigg(\sqrt{\frac{t}{N}}+\frac{t}{N}\bigg)}}\right]\leq 2e^{-t}.

Let
\mathcal{N}
be a
\frac{1}{2}
-net of
\mathbb{S}^{d-1}
with
|\mathcal{N}|\leq 5^{d}
.
By a union bound over
\mathcal{N}
and (
C.23
) with
t=d+\log(2/\delta)
,
we obtain with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\max_{u\in\mathcal{N}}\bigg|\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}W_{i}(u)\bigg|\leq C\big(\sigma_{Z}\|\Sigma_{XX}\|^{1/2}+\|\mu\|_{2}\big)\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\sqrt{\frac{d+\log(2/\delta)}{N}}+\frac{d+\log(2/\delta)}{N}}}\right).

Finally, a standard covering argument shows that for any
v\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
,

\|v\|_{2}\leq 2\max_{u\in\mathcal{N}}\langle u,v\rangle.

Applying this with

v=\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}X_{i}Z_{i}-\mu

yields the desired Euclidean-norm bound and completes the proof.
∎

Lemma C.5
.

Let
X\sim\mathcal{N}(0,\Sigma)
in
\mathbb{R}^{d}
with
cI_{d}\preceq\Sigma\preceq CI_{d}
for some constants
0<c\leq C<\infty
, and let
X_{1},\ldots,X_{N}
be i.i.d. copies of
X
. Define
\widehat{\Sigma}:=\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}X_{i}X_{i}^{\top}.
Fix
\delta\in(0,1)
. If
N\gtrsim(\frac{C}{c})^{2}(d+\log(1/\delta))
, then with probability at least
1-\delta/2
,

\sigma_{\min}(\widehat{\Sigma})\geq c/2,

and in particular
\widehat{\Sigma}
is invertible. Let
\Omega\subset\{1,\ldots,d\}
, and conditioned on
\widehat{\Sigma}
, let
v\sim\mathcal{N}(0,(\widehat{\Sigma}^{-1})_{\Omega,\Omega})
. Then, with probability at least
1-\delta
jointly over
(X_{1},\ldots,X_{N},v)
,

\|v\|_{\max}\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\log(|\Omega|/\delta)}{c}}.

Proof of Lemma
C.5
.

By the standard operator-norm deviation bound for Gaussian sample covariances
[
82
]
,

\mathbb{P}\bigg[\,\|\widehat{\Sigma}-\Sigma\|\lesssim\|\Sigma\|\Big(\sqrt{\tfrac{d+\log(2/\delta)}{N}}+\tfrac{d+\log(2/\delta)}{N}\Big)\bigg]\geq 1-\tfrac{\delta}{2}.

If
N\gtrsim(\frac{C}{c})^{2}(d+\log(1/\delta))
, the deviation term is at most
\frac{1}{2}\sigma_{\min}(\Sigma)
. By Weyl’s inequality,

\sigma_{\min}(\widehat{\Sigma})\geq\sigma_{\min}(\Sigma)-\|\widehat{\Sigma}-\Sigma\|\geq\frac{1}{2}\sigma_{\min}(\Sigma)\geq c/2

with probability at least
1-\delta/2
. Denote this event by
\mathcal{E}
; in particular,
\widehat{\Sigma}
is invertible on
\mathcal{E}
.

Condition on
\widehat{\Sigma}
and let
v\sim\mathcal{N}(0,(\widehat{\Sigma}^{-1})_{\Omega,\Omega})
.
Since
v=(v_{j})_{j\in\Omega}
has coordinates
v_{j}\sim\mathcal{N}(0,(\widehat{\Sigma}^{-1})_{jj})
for
j\in\Omega
, we have

\mathbb{P}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left[\|v\|_{\max}>t\,\big|\,\widehat{\Sigma}}}\right]\leq\sum_{j\in\Omega}2\exp\!\Big(-\tfrac{t^{2}}{2(\widehat{\Sigma}^{-1})_{jj}}\Big)\leq 2|\Omega|\exp\!\Big(-\tfrac{t^{2}\,\sigma_{\min}(\widehat{\Sigma})}{2}\Big).

Taking
t=\sqrt{\frac{2\log(4|\Omega|/\delta)}{\sigma_{\min}(\widehat{\Sigma})}}
gives conditional probability at most
\delta/2
. On
\mathcal{E}
,
\sigma_{\min}(\widehat{\Sigma})\geq c/2
, hence

\|v\|_{\max}\leq\sqrt{\frac{4\log(4|\Omega|/\delta)}{c}}\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\log(|\Omega|/\delta)}{c}}

with conditional probability at least
1-\delta/2
.

Intersecting the two events yields the result with probability at least
1-\delta
jointly over
(X_{1},\ldots,X_{N},v)
.
∎

Appendix D
Auxiliary Materials Section
6

We explain why the modified estimator defined through the updated support
\mathrm{supp}^{\mathrm{new}}(J,t,t^{\prime})
in (
6.1
) can yield the enhanced bound (
6.1
).

A direct modification of the proof of Proposition
5.10
gives

\mathsf{Error\text{-}Compression}\lesssim\varepsilon 2^{-J(t+t^{\prime}-r)}.

Indeed, recall that

\mathsf{Error\text{-}Compression}=\|\mathbf{D}^{-t^{\prime}}_{\Lambda_{J}}(\mathbf{A}^{\varepsilon}_{\Lambda_{J}}-\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{J}})\mathbf{D}^{-t}_{\Lambda_{J}}\|\leq\bigg(\sum_{j,j^{\prime}=j_{0}}^{J}2^{-2jt^{\prime}-2j^{\prime}t}\|(\mathbf{A}^{\varepsilon}_{\Lambda_{J}})_{j,j^{\prime}}-(\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{J}})_{j,j^{\prime}}\|^{2}\bigg)^{1/2}.

For indices
(j,j^{\prime})
where the new thresholding rule is performed, we have

\displaystyle\|(\mathbf{A}^{\varepsilon}_{\Lambda_{J}})_{j,j^{\prime}}-(\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{J}})_{j,j^{\prime}}\|\lesssim 2^{-(j+j^{\prime})\widetilde{d}}\cdot(\tau_{jj^{\prime}}^{\mathrm{new}})^{-(2\widetilde{d}+r)}\leq\frac{\varepsilon}{J}2^{-J(t+t^{\prime}-r)+jt^{\prime}+j^{\prime}t},

and for discarded indices

\displaystyle\|(\mathbf{A}^{\varepsilon}_{\Lambda_{J}})_{j,j^{\prime}}-(\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{J}})_{j,j^{\prime}}\|=\|(\mathbf{A}_{\Lambda_{J}})_{j,j^{\prime}}\|\lesssim 2^{(j+j^{\prime})r/2-|j-j^{\prime}|(\sigma-n/2)}\leq\frac{\varepsilon}{J}2^{-J(t+t^{\prime}-r)+jt^{\prime}+j^{\prime}t}.

Substituting these bounds above yields the claimed estimate for
\mathsf{Error\text{-}Compression}
.

Next, observe that the new thresholding parameters and slope conditions differ from those in (
4.2
) only by polynomial factors in
J
and by at most
O(\log J)
shifts in scale. Consequently, the arguments in Proposition
5.11
and Proposition
5.12
carry over with minor changes.

For Proposition
5.11
, the modifications consist of verifying the support-inclusion relations (as in Lemma
B.1
) under the new thresholding rule and slope conditions, and choosing an appropriately adjusted
\widetilde{J}
. This gives

\mathsf{Error\text{-}OVB}\lesssim\varepsilon 2^{-J(t+t^{\prime}-r)}.

For the variance term, one adapts the support-pattern analysis in Proposition
C.2
to the new support
\mathrm{supp}^{\mathrm{new}}(J,t,t^{\prime})
. With this in place, the proof of Proposition
5.12
extends directly and yields

\mathsf{Error\text{-}Var}\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\delta)}{N}}\cdot(J/\varepsilon)^{\kappa}\cdot 2^{\rho J(t+t^{\prime}-r)/2},

for some constant
\kappa>0
, where
\rho
is the same exponent as in (
5.2
).
We omit the technical details for brevity.

Finally, combining the bounds for
\mathsf{Error\text{-}Compression},\mathsf{Error\text{-}OVB}
, and
\mathsf{Error\text{-}Var}
yields the estimate (
6.1
).
